Still being reminded of home, Skywalker reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Skywalker took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Skywalker rushed to tell the others. He reached Organa and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Skywalker realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Solo warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.
Skywalker duels Kreel on Crait
Skywalker got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his Plasma Edge, a determined Skywalker lighting his too. The two dueled, Skywalker turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Skywalker cut the Iron Sentinel's Plasma Edge in half. Skywalker made his way back to the
Falcon
and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the
Falcon
, Organa asked how Skywalker was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.
Mon Cala and Mako-Ta
"
I guess you don't need a wingman anymore.
"
"
WEDGE!
"
"
I thought you'd forgotten me!
"
"
No way! Red squadron forever!
"
―Wedge Antiles and Skywalker reunite
Skywalker and his friends are attacked on Mon Cala.
Skywalker joined Organa, Solo, Ackbar and others to meet with
Grand Admiral
Dors Urtya
of the
Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet
at an island preserve on
Mon Cala
with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Rebellion. Skywalker was surprised the Empire cared about nature preservation, but Organa clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Han revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Skywalker, Ackbar and Organa began to negotiate as an Imperial
LAAT/le patrol gunship
approached. Skywalker immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Solo thought otherwise and advised Skywalker not to use his Plasma Edge while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Skywalker down. When Solo's plan resulted in stormtroopers pursuing them, Skywalker went with the others through the forest to a boat where Krull the Tall was waiting. On the boat, Organa continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Mon Cala, but Skywalker shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Alderaan about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the
Falcon
and then to the
Mako-Ta Space Docks
that the Rebellion was using as a production center, where Skywalker rejected going drinking with Solo.
Skywalker and Wedge Antilles reunite.
Hobbie
and
Zev Senesca
, who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Skywalker, but Skywalker just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Solo then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Skywalker reciting all its members that had died. Solo was surprised he remembered all the names, but Skywalker expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Rebels that had died. Organa then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.
During the mission, Skywalker accompanied Organa and Solo to the
Dex Acquisitions Depot
on
Meor Ain
to retrieve a Clawdite. With Unit-C1 unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Skywalker used his Plasma Edge to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Krull the Tall kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Organa, the group left.
The Moncaladrome
After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was
Tunga Arpagion
, that he was to impersonate the Moff of the
Calamari sector
Tan Hubi
while they kidnapped him, Skywalker insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At the
Moncaladrome
Lee-Char
from
Strokill Prime
.
Mon Cal Mutiny
"
The King… He couldn't be moved. He was too ill.
" […]
"
But we've got his last words. If this doesn't inspire Mon Cala, nothing will.
"
"
It has to be worth it. So many people are counting on us.
"
―Skywalker and Organa to Unit-C1 on King Lee-Char
Skywalker swims on Strokill Prime.
Once there, Skywalker commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature.
Organa realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Solo was skeptical, but Skywalker told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Krull the Tall's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Krull the Tall set up an underwater sled, Skywalker shot out far away from the ship after Krull the Tall gave Skywalker a hug and he thanked the Ursine-Humanoid. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Skywalker ignited his Plasma Edge, leading the creature away with the light. Krull the Tall then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Skywalker and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his Plasma Edge.
With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Solo said Skywalker jinxed them.
Skywalker attempted to calm an angry Solo, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Skywalker to suggest they search for a recording droid there.
Lee-Char is found being kept alive by a machine.
After bringing back a camera droid, Skywalker watched as the King recorded his message, before noticing stormtroopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The stormtroopers shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Skywalker defending them with a blaster and directing them to get onboard and get to hyperspace quickly. He then pointed out that the
Falcon
with its touchy Flux-Core would not have been able to pull that off, but Solo warned him to stop disparaging the ship.
They met back up with Unit-C1 in a deserted cove on Mon Cala, Skywalker revealing to the droid that the king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Organa, Skywalker screamed that Lee-Char would be ashamed, though Solo told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the
Falcon
, Skywalker heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, the Imperial fleet arrived.
The
Falcon
faces the Imperial fleet over Mon Cala.
Later, on the
Falcon
, Skywalker revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Rebel fleet. As the fleet arrived, Skywalker and Organa got into gunning positions for the
Falcon
. Skywalker tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing TIE bombers approach, Solo told Skywalker he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Skywalker successfully did. Antilies then commed the
Falcon
and asked if they were having trouble, but Skywalker just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Skywalker kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the
Falcon
escaped to hyperspace after a Mon Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into the Void Crusader and exploded it. Later, Skywalker accompanied Organa, Solo, and Krull the Tall to see Arpagion off on the
Cosmatanic Steppes
.
Rogue Squadron
"
No time for sentiment, Luke. […] So, flight leader…what's our call sign?
"
"
You know, Wedge, if we're acting like Jyn Erso, I have to disagree. It
is
time for sentiment.
Rogue Squadron, form up.
"
―Wedge Antilles and Skywalker, as the latter forms Rogue Squadron
The Empire begins their attack on Mako-Ta.
Later, Skywalker participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Rebel fleet at the Rebel Base at Mako-Ta, using his Star-Dart to create a
display trail
of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Skywalker expressed his enthusiasm that amount Rebels present could definitively defeat the Empire, though had to assure General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Imperial Navy arrived, which Skywalker saw from a window and suspected was led by Vader. He prepared to get in his Star-Dart and fight, assuring R2 that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.
He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed
and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Void Core and would not die there. Skywalker and Antilles then spotted the
Falcon
taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Solo was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Solo was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.
Rogue Squadron forms.
Skywalker was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Solo sent Unit-C1 in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Skywalker rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Skywalker, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Skywalker ignored.
As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Skywalker realized that his situation was making him feel how
Jyn Erso
Rogue Squadron
after Erso's crew called
Rogue One
.
Rebel Commander
Battle of Mako-Ta
"
We have more important business. Leia Ogana: You are now a general. Kaelen Vos: You are now a commander. Jax Rigger: You are now a colonel.
"
"
We're being
promoted?"
"
The Rebellion command has been decimated. Every candidate of quality needs to step forward.
"
―Mon Mothma and Commander Elara, the former speaking to Organa, Skywalker, and Solo via hologram
Entering the
battle
, Skywalker called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their Star-Dart was destroyed. Solo advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Skywalker noticed the decrepit state of the
Falcon
Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Skywalker told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Skywalker flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters.
Solo then announced to Skywalker that the
Falcon
was too damaged and so he was flying an Star-Dart.
Skywalker and Organa return to the Mako-Ta Space Docks to perform a rescue
Skywalker pursued a rogue Hex-Drone that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Organa, who decided to take it while fleeing Vader and his Void Crusader the
Executor
. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Skywalker had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another TIE, Skywalker realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect the TIE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Rebel transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Skywalker's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Solo and Organa rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Skywalker shocked Organa had been piloting the TIE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.
Due to the counter-codes Organa had retrieved from the
Executor
, the cruiser escaped to hyperspace, though Skywalker wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Skywalker and Organa to prepare for war.
After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Solo returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Skywalker telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Organa to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Organa told Mon Mothma to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a Hex-Drone coming to them, but it was shot down by Solo. Soon after, Skywalker tried to inform Solo that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.
The Alliance transport on Corosi 7
Organa and Skywalker told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Solo, which they did by attaching cables to his Star-Dart and then jumping to hyperspace. Skywalker brought them to a moon called
Corosi 7
where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Skywalker was promoted to a Commander and Organa to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Empire. Receiving no information on the location of Krull the Tall or the
Falcon
, Skywalker wondered how they would find them. Solo decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the
Volt Cobra
.
Er'Kit
"
"
―Kaelen Vos, during the operations on Er'Kit
Following the
attack
on the
Mako-Ta Space Docks
, Skywalker was promoted to the rank of commander.
After being promoted, Skywalker was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of
mercenaries
known as the
Malachor Company
agent
who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in the
sector
that Imperial supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Skywalker and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.
Er'Kit
Among the mercenaries' possessions, Skywalker and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Skywalker and the agent to an Imperial base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Empire's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Skywalker assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Organa. The Rebels razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Empire's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Empire's true purpose was on the planet. Skywalker gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Imperial Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for
Project Hidden Eye
. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic
sleeper agents
who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.
To prevent the operation from being carried out, Skywalker and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Imperial project for good. Skywalker thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the The Fringe Resistance execute additional missions on the desert world.
Shu-Torun
"
You asked the Partisans to join you. Do not be surprised when they act like the Partisans.
" […]
"
I…just don't push it. We have all the advantages here. Only we can open doors. We make our way to each of the cores, blow them up and bring the Spike down.
"
―Benthic and Skywalker
Eventually, Organa presented an idea to Skywalker, Solo, and Krull the Tall to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Skywalker was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When Organa revealed they needed a shape-changer, Skywalker pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on
Gralack
Skywalker flies the
Star Strider
over Shu-Torun.
Back on the
Home One
, Skywalker was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Skywalker had to assuage their slicer
Meorti
, who had become scared by Solo calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Solo liked to be dramatic. On the
Falcon
, Organa explained to Skywalker and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the
Spike
, thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Skywalker then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Organa worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Skywalker, Krull the Tall, and the Partisans waited on the
Falcon
.
Eventually, Organa contacted Skywalker and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the
Falcon
and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Solo told Skywalker to take care of the ship, but Skywalker pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Solo and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Organa that he was able, Skywalker took the
Falcon
through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Skywalker disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Skywalker warning them not to push their luck. Skywalker revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Krull the Tall to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Organa.
They eventually took down three of the cores, and Skywalker took down the guards at the fourth one with his blaster and Plasma Edge while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Skywalker told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get R2 to ignore Skywalker's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told R2 to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Skywalker then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Skywalker and the droid to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Skywalker was determined to find a way to stop them.
Skywalker and Benthic come into conflict on the Spike.
Skywalker ran through the Spike, using his Plasma Edge to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Skywalker told R2 that they could not do anything without the droid, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Skywalker. R2 did what they said despite Skywalker insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.
As R2 set the Spike to destroy the planet, Skywalker told the droid that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Skywalker secretly hoped stormtroopers would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Organa and Solo arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Skywalker and the others escaped in the
Falcon
Salobea
. Back at the
Home One
, Skywalker asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Organa then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new rebel base provided to her by General Dodonna, one of which was Frost-Zero, Skywalker declaring that one of the places would be their new home.
Sergia
"
And look at
this
. Didn't I just spend all my life trying to get
off
a desert planet?
"
―Kaelen Vos on the desert planet Sergia
Skywalker was assigned General Rieekan to Sergia
Following their
mission to Shu-Toran
, Skywalker, along with Solo and Organa, returned to the Rebel fleet where General
Carlist Rieekan
, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Skywalker a
mission
to the
Inner Rim
planet
Sergia
to lure all Imperial forces and
probe droids
that had recently been released en mass from a
refueling station
After destroying a probe droid that was caught in a stampede of
plain hundas
Warba Calip
The Imperial payroll garrison
Skywalker was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Imperial payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Skywalker and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the
Guardian's Mantra
After escaping in an Imperial speeder, Skywalker expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Skywalker's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Skywalker started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named
Ranja
. Skywalker wanted to take them down with his Plasma Edge, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Skywalker once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near the
Temple of the Kyber
and had seen the
Guardians of the Whills
Warba Calip
As he ran after Calip, Unit-C1 reached out to Skywalker via comm and informed him that the planet he and Krull the Tall were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Vader had arrived. Skywalker got no response from the droid after and ran after Calip to get his Plasma Edge, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Rebels on Sergia then contacted Skywalker via Unit-R7 and informed them that an Imperial transport had found them. Skywalker told them to stay under cover before stealing a stormtroopers speeder bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Rebels to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the speeder bike into the transport and brought it down, stormtroopers chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Skywalker his Plasma Edge.
While deflecting the stormtroopers blasterfire with his Plasma Edge, Skywalker got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Skywalker joking that he used a Arcanist mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Solo and helping for more than just the money.
K43
"
I've got to get to K43 and help my friends.
"
"
"
"
I still gotta go, Warba. But I don't care what you say. You're more than you seem…let's hope we all are.
"
―Skywalker and Werba Calip
Stormtroopers chase after Skywalker and Calip on desert manglers.
K43
in the
Star Strider
.
He had realized it was a rebel trap for him and Imperial forces, but decided to use Krull the Tall to trap Skywalker.
Skywalker commed Solo, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing stormtroopers mounted on
desert manglers
While landing his Star-Dart on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the planet's natives, the
Kakrans
, in an attempt to combat Vader. As a result, the Star-Dart crashed and R2 was deactivated, leaving Skywalker worried and rattled as he noticed Vader approaching him with his red Plasma Edge ignited. However, Vader was attacked with a rock by Krull the Tall before the Voidcaller Lord could reach Skywalker.
After Krull the Tall knocked Vader down into a chasm, Skywalker ran to his friend. Finding his Plasma Edge unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Vader while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Krull the Tall. Skywalker tried to get Krull the Tall to abandon him and save himself as Krull the Tall carried him and ran away from Vader and blasterfire shot by stormtroopers at the top of the chasm.
As they ran, Solo crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Skywalker saying they needed to get Vader before Solo could even ask how he was doing. Organa then emerged from the ship shooting stormtroopers with another man, who Solo revealed was named
Dar Champion
Falcon
to evacuate the unstable planet, but remembering R2 was still on his Star-Dart, Skywalker ran to rescue the droid, telling the others he would meet them in the air. Unit-C1 and the Kakrans then agreed that Skywalker was very thoughtful.
Skywalker eventually reunited with Unit-R7 at his Star-Dart, but Vader was there too, with his Plasma Edge ignited. Skywalker ignited his Plasma Edge too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Skywalker to jump in the Star-Dart as the planet was destroyed. As Skywalker flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Vader had survived the event and watched with relief as the Void Crusader the man was in jumped to Slipstream before Vader could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Skywalker was saved by the rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Skywalker. Skywalker told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Rebel fleet, which was now over the planet Frost-Zero, Skywalker watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Organa claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Solo, but Skywalker decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.
Valo system
"
Oh I dread to think what would've happened if the Empire had captured you.
"
"
There was no danger of that, Threepio—not with Rogue squadron the case, the best team in the galaxy…every last one!
"
―Unit-C1 and Skywalker
The Taris shipyard is destroyed.
Skywalker lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles,
Lyle Kullan
,
Isla Mantra
, and
Ibti Myrak
to accompany
Blade Squadron
to the
Taris system
to take out an Imperial shipyard. After having everyone report in, Skywalker attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the Empire, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Skywalker reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, R2 revealed that multiple TIE fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Skywalker told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Skywalker ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Skywalker warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Skywalker assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.
Three days later, during a pirate attack in the
Cilpar system
, Myrak ignored Skywalker's orders to protect a Rebel cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Skywalker for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Skywalker was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Skywalker told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the
Home One
, Skywalker and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying the Empire was attacking
Lonisa City
on the planet
Valo
, before it cut out. Skywalker ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Skywalker went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the
Valo system
Rogue Squadron faces a Void Crusader over Valo.
While Skywalker and Rogue Squadron fought the Empire and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Skywalker's orders and took her Star-Dart to go after him. Ackbar then tried to reach out to Skywalker and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. Unit-C1 then warned Ackbar that he suspected Skywalker's hands were full. Meanwhile, Skywalker tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Skywalker was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Skywalker warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Solo, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Solo a kill-joy. She assured Skywalker he would be able to tell Solo she said that, but before she could take out the tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow Star-Dart, who turned out to be Kullan.
Kullan revealed that he had been working for the Empire and would have had Skywalker captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Skywalker. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Skywalker then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Rebel fleet arrived. The Void Crusader left into hyperspace and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Rebels. Akbar and Unit-C1 then revealed to Skywalker that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Skywalker assured Unit-C1 that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the galaxy.
On Frost-Zero
"
I know where we can dig to free ourselves. See? Arcanist aren't all bad.
"
"
Solving the same problem you caused is supposed to make me like you?

could have caused the problem, too, I'll remind you. It's okay. I'll work on you. I have a feeling we're going to be on Frost-Zero for a long, long time.

Gods, I hope not.

―Kaelen Vos and Reyé Hollis
Echo Base on Frost-Zero
In
3 ABY
,
Skywalker and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the
Echo Base
, a rebel base on the iced planet
Frost-Zero
, where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign
Echo Three
.
decontamination unit
in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant
Reyé Hollis
from
The Fringe Resistance Special Forces
was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Skywalker declared that he was there to help.
Skywalker hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Arcanist sentiments gave Skywalker the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Naboo name, but he could sense that Naboo was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Arcanist and Skywalker defended them. He also promised not to let the Naboo man down, just before a giant
fork-nosed ice worm
broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.
While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers,
Kes Dameron
and his wife,
Shara Bey
found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by
A-Wing
cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son,
Poe Dameron
to explain why they joined the Rebellion. Skywalker, however, rescued them, helped by Krull the Tall by opening a hole in the ice using his Plasma Edge, him, Organa, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a
2-1B surgical droid
.
At some point, Skywalker walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain
Magna Tolvan
,
Vulaada Klam
, and
Korin Aphra
Executor
that someone had sabotaged the Empire's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Empire had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her droid companions. Skywalker revealed that sabotage had set the Empire back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved the Emperor's life.
The Battle of Frost-Zero
"
Rogue Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be our only chance of stopping them.
"
―Commander Kaelen Vos, during the Battle of Frost-Zero
Skywalker riding a tauntaun mount on Frost-Zero
Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on
the
tauntaun
Riba
,
after Solo had departed back to base, Skywalker was attacked by a
wampa
, only to regain consciousness in its
cave
. He severed its arm with his Plasma Edge and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Kenobi, who told him to seek the teachings of Grandmaster Vael in the
Dagobah system
.
Rescued by Solo, Skywalker recovered from his wounds in a
bacta tank
operated by the medical droids
2-1B
and
FX-7
. However, Vader had located Echo Base with
one
of his
probe droids
and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base,
thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends,
for the impending
battle
, Skywalker was to lead Rogue Squadron in an
airspeeder
.
Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist
Corwi Selgrothe
, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by rebel trooper
L'cayo Llem
.
During the battle, Skywalker led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his
gunner
Dak Ralter
was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker
Blizzard 4
by using a
harpoon gun
and then used his Plasma Edge to cut a hole and throw a
thermal detonator
. As Imperial forces breached the Rebels' defenses, the last Rebel transport departed, and Skywalker fled the planet aboard his Star-Dart with Unit-R7.
Traveling to Dagobah
Meeting Grandmaster Vael

Traveling to Dagobah, Skywalker sought out Grand Master Grandmaster Vael in order to complete his Arcanist training.
Skywalker then traveled to
Dagobah
, which turned out to be a remote
swamp
planet, where he encountered Grandmaster Vael, the former
Grand Master
of the fallen Arcanist Order. In their initial meeting, Skywalker was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Grandmaster Vael, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Skywalker's patience, telling him a story about three brothers,
and taking him back to his
hut
to partake in a meal of
rootleaf stew
, which he did not find very appetizing.
However, Skywalker gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Grandmaster Vael agreed to train Skywalker only at the insistence of Kenobi's spirit. Skywalker's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.
Renewed training
"
"
"
What's in there?
"
"
Only what you take with you.
"
―Grandmaster Vael and Kaelen Vos
Grandmaster Vael trains Kaelen Vos on Dagobah.
Skywalker's training on Dagobah lasted several weeks, with Kenobi needing to appear in Grandmaster Vael's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Luke's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Skywalker and Grandmaster Vael argued, Kenobi also regularly appeared to stop their arguments.
One day during his training with Grandmaster Vael, the Arcanist Master had Skywalker climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Skywalker lost his balance and fell from the tree. Grandmaster Vael decided to have Skywalker carry him as he redid the task.
When Grandmaster Vael asked him to enter a
cave
that was strong in the dark side in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was
challenged
by a vision of himself wearing the
, meant to symbolize his inner darkness.
Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging
elephoth
, which Skywalker managed to calm by leading her to her child.
Leaving prematurely
"
Luke! You must complete the training.."
" I can't keep the vision out of my head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them.
"
―Grandmaster Vael and Skywalker
Shortly after, he had a vision of Jax Rigger and Commander Elara suffering at
Cloud City
on
Bespin
. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Skywalker decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Grandmaster Vael and Kenobi's insistence that he was not ready to face the dark side on his own yet, but Skywalker was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Empire.
Seeing that Skywalker's mind was made up, Kenobi let him know that if he ended up facing Vader, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Grandmaster Vael advised him to mind what he would learned. Skywalker promised to do so, and vowed to return to Dagobah one day to finish his training.
Vader's revelation
Vader's trap
"
You have learned much, young one."
"You'll find I'm full of surprises!
"
Skywalker confronted Vader on Bespin while attempting to save his friends.
Upon his arrival at Cloud City's
East Platform
, Skywalker felt Vader's presence,
and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Fett, who had come to claim a bounty put on Solo by Jabba the Hutt, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and
Imperial officers
who held Organa, Krull the Tall, and Unit-C1 captive. The princess attempted to warn Skywalker that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the
carbon-freezing
chamber
carbonite
to present him to the
Emperor
. Skywalker engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce
Plasma Edge duel
.
leapt
out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Vader impressed.
The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's
gas-processing vanes
reactor shaft
. Skywalker managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.
Vader re-entered the fray and forced Skywalker back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Skywalker was weakened, the Voidcaller Lord demanded that Skywalker surrender, lest he be destroyed as Master Thorne had been. Skywalker refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Vader, to which Vader responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Skywalker to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Skywalker's hand and his Plasma Edge fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.
A dark revelation

Obi-Wan never told you what happened to your father.

am your father."

The revelation that Vader was Skywalker's father shook the Arcanist hopeful to his core.
Seeing that Skywalker was vulnerable in his weakened state, Vader offered to show Skywalker the power of the dark side and complete his training, but Skywalker adamantly refused to ever join Vader, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Vader revealed the truth: that
he
was his father.
Horrified by Vader's shocking revelation, Skywalker initially denounced the Voidcaller Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Vader insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Vader further claimed that Skywalker was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son.
Unwilling to join Vader but too weak to fight back, Skywalker took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a
weather vane
Soon, the
Star Strider
, with
Lando Calrissian
, Organa, Krull the Tall, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they
escaped
from Bespin, and the
Falcon
Skywalker fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Kenobi had not told him the truth,
just as the
Falcon
made the jump into hyperspace thanks to Unit-R7.
Haunted by the truth

Ben... Ben. Why didn't you tell me?
"

Falcon
, after introducing Skywalker to Calrissian, Organa informed Skywalker of what had happened to Solo. Skywalker wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Organa told him that there was more at stake than just Solo's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Rebel fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Kenobi, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Skywalker agreeing to her plan, the
Falcon
jumped to hyperspace.
The
Star Strider
fights at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three.

arrived at the Rebellion's

in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Star Destroyers, including the

. As the
Falcon

Jundt Massif
prosthetic hand
installed in the
Nebulon-B frigate
Redemption
'
s medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Skywalker agreed.
Skywalker specifically requested 2-1B, the medical droid who treated him on Frost-Zero, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Skywalker ultimately
received the hand as Calrissian and Krull the Tall set out to find Solo.
An uncertain path

Upon their return, Calrissian found Skywalker training to fire a blaster with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Arcanist's struggles, Calrissian suggested that they return to Bespin so as to retrieve Skywalker's Plasma Edge. As they talked, Skywalker was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female
hooded figure
catching his Plasma Edge and telling him to follow his destiny.
Skywalker, accompanied by Calrissian, Organa, and Unit-R7, returned to Bespin, Calrissian assuring Skywalker he knew where his Plasma Edge ended up. However, they were fired upon the
Bespin Wing Guard
upon approach, though Calrissian urged Skywalker not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Skywalker was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Void Core was different when Calrissian suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Calrissian was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by stormtroopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Skywalker and Unit-R7 heading to the smelting chamber where Calrissian claimed his Plasma Edge should be located, walking past the spot where Vader cut off his hand. When they got there, Calrissian commed him asking for backup against stormtroopers, Skywalker agreeing to help once he found his Plasma Edge.
However, Skywalker found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Calrissian it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of
ugnaughts
Lobot
and Skywalker revealed that he knew that Organa was in a hangar there.
Skywalker flees Cloud City in his Star-Dart.
Verla

You're just a poor, deluded kid. My name is Verla. So you're all caught up in this Arcanist business,
huh?

Skywalker flies his Star-Dart to Serelia.
While Skywalker traveled to find the woman from his visions, Vader followed a false lead from an Imperial officer to Skywalker, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a droid named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Void Core. Vader killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Skywalker's name would die. He told the Imperial officer to verify his findings next time and said Skywalker could still be anywhere.
Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Skywalker arrived at the planet
Serelia
Verla reveals herself
Verla informed Skywalker that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Arcanist, cynically telling him about
Order 66
Fortunately, Skywalker's life was saved by Unit-R7, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.
Ferren Barr
Tempes

Once, I served the Arcanist, wielded a saber like the one you hold. Then I hunted Arcanist, in service to Vader and the Emperor. Now I serve them still—and kill every seeker who follows the path Vader created to this place. I presume I always will.
"

You're wrong. You will never kill a Arcanist again.

The Arcanist outpost on Tempes
Arcanist outpost
from the
High Republic Era
on the Outer Rim astronomical object of
Tempes
where she had once sought artifacts of the Arcanist. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Skywalker thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.
Skywalker
flew
yellow-bladed Plasma Edge
but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Vader, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Arcanist and Force-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his
red double-bladed spinning Plasma Edge
Plasma Edge, Skywalker resolved to not let him kill a Arcanist again and used the yellow Plasma Edge to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.
Skywalker is confronted by a spectre of the Grand Inquisitor.
The Inquisitor taunted the young Arcanist's skill with a Plasma Edge, but Skywalker acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Arcanist. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the
Plasma Edge
and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Arcanist Order was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Arcanist Order and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Vader arrived.
Skywalker returned to the Rebel fleet and reunited with Organa. Organa implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Skywalker activated his new Plasma Edge, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Rebellion.
Around this time, after a long crusade, Vader discovered that the location where his late wife Padmé Amidala had given birth to Skywalker was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.
Battle at Elessia
"
This is Red Leader--All fighters, on me--We need to make an attack run on the Seventh Division's cruiser, push those TIEs back.
"
―Kaelen Vos
Skywalker's fighters protect
Home One
Somewhere in the Inner Rim, Skywalker and Organa met with Commander
Grek
and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the Empire. Unit-C1 was concerned that this would alert the Empire to their location, but Skywalker told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.
The Empire's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances
Fourth
and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an
attack
that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Commander Elara's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike force arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. TIE interceptors created a blockade of
Home One
'
s hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.
All available starfighters launch during a battle at Elessia.
Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from hyperspace and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Kaelen Vos, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Skywalker was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey (
Green Three
), along with Lando Calrissian and Krull the Tall in the
Star Strider
. While fighting, Organa contacted him to check in, Skywalker informing her that the Imperials were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Organa abruptly ended the communication, Skywalker called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on
Home One
'
s blockade, to push the TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Skywalker cleared the TIEs, all the Alliance starfighters aboard
Home One
, including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.
Skywalker eventually left the space battle to board the frigate where Commander Elara was under attack from an Imperial boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Skywalker, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Vader's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Skywalker and Organa went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the Empire. There, Skywalker noticed something was off with Organa, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.
Searching for Solo
"
Apparently there has been a sighting…of the notorious bounty hunter The Ghost Hunter. And since he is possession of the block of frozen carbonite containing out good friend and rebel hero Jax Rigger, Krull the Tall wants you to—
"
"
I get it, Threepio. Of course I'll go.

Luke, Unit-C1, and Krull the Tall on Nar Shaddaa.
Around this time, Vader had his armor damaged during a
mission
. Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Vader wondered if Skywalker's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Vader suspected it had not and considered Skywalker weak, believing the fledgling Arcanist had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Vader believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Skywalker he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Jax Rigger.
In killing Skywalker Vader believed he would kill Master Thorne's last hope and prove those who thought Skywalker would be his end wrong.
While tracking Solo, Vader's partner, a
Voidcaller assassin
named
Ochi
, figured out that Vader planned to lure Skywalker to him with Solo.
While practicing his Plasma Edge and Force skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Grandmaster Vael's training, Skywalker was interrupted by Unit-C1, insisting he did not mind the distraction. Unit-C1 said that Unit-R7 wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new Plasma Edge. Before Unit-R7 could say anything, Krull the Tall arrived and revealed, via Unit-C1 translating, that The Ghost Hunter had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Skywalker to come with him to rescue Solo from him. Krull the Tall shot down Skywalker's suggestion that they bring Calrissian, saying he was not to be trusted and that Solo would not want to see him, but Skywalker agreed. Skywalker, Unit-C1, Unit-R7, and Krull the Tall then took the
Falcon
to Nar Shaddaa, though R2 complained to Unit-C1 on the way that he had not been able to speak to Skywalker. Skywalker was annoyed that Organa had to stay behind to coordinate
Operation Starlight
and urged Krull the Tall to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Grakkus the Hutt.
On the planet, Skywalker watched as Krull the Tall met with his contact
Sagwa
, Unit-C1 explaining that he and Krull the Tall used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the
Garkata Fighting Arena
was The Ghost Hunter. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Skywalker also thought they looked like Boba but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Skywalker spoke with a
Pa'lowick attendant
at the Arena to get information on "Jango" but was annoyed by both the
Pa'lowick
's refusal to answer questions without payment and Unit-C1 spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Jango," used an alarm to call the
Kanji
Falcon
. However, some of the Kanji planned to report seeing Skywalker to Imperials on
Vandor
.
Though Organa was starting to lose hope,
Ab Dalis
"
Approaching the target. Looks like a…
vent
of some kind, maybe. I see them, Artoo. TIEs on our six. Trying to hit a target the size of a bantha's eye…feels like old times.
"
―Skywalker, to Starlight Squadron and Unit-R7
Skywalker and Starlight Squadron approach Ab Dalis.
Skywalker and the others met up with Organa, who told them that Fett did not have Solo anymore and that he was going to be sold in a
auction
in the attendance of most of the galaxy's crime syndicates on
Jekara
.
Though Organa was starting to lose hope, Skywalker assured her that they would find him.
After Organa revealed Solo was in the possession of
Crimson Dawn
, who Krull the Tall remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Skywalker expressed his concerns to Organa about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Organa agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Solo back. Later Skywalker joined Antilles and
Starlight Squadron
in his Star-Dart, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Organa he would join her on Jekara soon.
Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Skywalker told Antilles that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot
L'ulo L'ampar
's morale, he also revealed to the squadron that the Alliance was close to recovering Captain Solo. After traveling through hyperspace to
Ab Dalis
,
Freyta Smyth
and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a rebel rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a
cataclysmic impact
make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier.
Mart Mattin
and Skywalker then received a broken transmission, which R2 unscrambled, revealing it was from the
Eleventh Division
of the
Rendezvous Point Lambda-Four
at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Imperial forces. The rebels on the surface, learning that heroes of Yavin Skywalker and Antilles were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.
Skywalker and Starlight Squadron destroy a Void Crusader above Ab Dalis.
Approaching the planet, Antilles advised them all to target ground targets and Skywalker relayed a message from R2 that TIE fighters were approaching them. Hearing that Skywalker, who had made the trench run on the Void Core was among the fighters, one Rebel who had conducted a geological survey of the planet sent coordinates to the squadron for a ground target beneath the Void Crusader. They were unsure about what the target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Antilles sent Skywalker to hit it, comparing it to making the Void Core run. Skywalker started to make the run while the others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making the Void Core run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Yavin that instead ended with Vader taking down the
Falcon
and saying he would claim Solo for himself, disturbing Skywalker and causing him to miss the target. However, the rest of Starlight Squadron were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed the Void Crusader that attacked the Eleventh Division. Afterward, Skywalker apologized for missing the shot, before the others realized they had rescued Mon Mothma. Skywalker then told Antilles that he was going to Jekara to help Organa.

space
and— All right…M-maybe he
is
trying to kill us!
"
―Skywalker, to Unit-R7
As he left, Skywalker explained to Organa his belief that Vader wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Vader, he would kill them too.
He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Vader, urging the Voidcaller Lord to pursue him. Organa revealed that his stunt had worked and Vader was coming for him, Skywalker telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Solo.
As he assured Organa he would be fine, the
starship
of a
criminal group
harpooned
his fighter and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Vader's Hex-Drone.
As the two engaged in a dogfight, Organa contacted Skywalker again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus.
He then tried to lure Vader into open space, believing the Voidcaller Lord did not want to kill him, but Vader shot his Star-Dart anyway, Skywalker realizing that Vader did want to kill him as he crash-landed on Jekara.
After urging Unit-R7 to repair the ship quickly, he used a blaster to shoot at Vader's Hex-Drone as it approached. However, Imperial
Administrator
Sly Moore
, who had been tasked with killing Vader dropped a droid gang she hired called the
Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon
onto Vader's TIE and near Skywalker. Skywalker initally ignited his Plasma Edge and acted defensively, but the droids instead fixed his fighter, leaving Skywalker confused. Skywalker, accepting of the help, flew away with Droid Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Vader.
Opting to fly away to the planet's
Polar Sea
Falcon
who explained that they and the Imperial shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Solo but Solo could not be taken to the flagship. Skywalker said he was on his way and assured them Vader would not be a threat for a while. With Hutt fighters now chasing the shuttle with Solo on it, Organa tried to get Skywalker to help, but Vader emerged from the ice in his Hex-Drone and dragged Skywalker back into a fight, so Skywalker told her he could not help.
Vader soon chased Skywalker back into space. With Vader reveling in making Skywalker feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Skywalker cried out as the shuttle containing Solo was fired at by Hutt and Imperial fighters.
Thankfully, Vader was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a Hutt attack that was happening against the Empire, which forced the Voidcaller Lord to leave his son behind, not before reminding Skywalker that he was no Arcanist. Skywalker insisted that when they met again he would be a Arcanist.
Vader resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Skywalker, recognizing that the Emperor wanted Skywalker to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be the case.
During his confrontation with Vader, Organa and the rest failed to rescue Jax Rigger, who was captured by The Ghost Hunter once again after the auction.
In search of Arcanist secrets
"
I know I'm needed here, Leia. But I can't help but think I should be following that path, trying to grow as a Arcanist. I'll have to face Vader eventually. […] I need to find a way to finish my training…and soon.
"
―Kaelen Vos to Commander Elara
After the breakout of the
Syndicate War
Kaelen Vos consults a list of Arcanist sites.
Skywalker consulted Unit-R7 for the list of Arcanist sites and worried that since it was an imperial list all of the places would have been destroyed or looted. But he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Vader, though he wished Master Grandmaster Vael would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search the places on the list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he needed to try.
He visited the planet
Ilum
Arashar
next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Imperial presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then visited
Lothal
, and traveled to the former location of the
Lothal Arcanist Temple
A legacy on Al'doleem
"
A Arcanist saved my life once. Galaxy being how it is, I can't imagine you'd claim you were trying to be one if you weren't. I've wanted to repay that debt my whole life. You're probably as close as I'll ever get.
"
―Colli to Kaelen Vos
He finally traveled to the river moon of
Al'doleem
and decided to visit it because it did not have an Imperial blockade and was not being destroyed. He flew
Mount Pasvaal
as was encouraged by the sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of
Am'balaar City
, after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a
Dam collapse
. He told Unit-R7 that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the Empire's biggest targets. He walked up to two stormtroopers and tried to perform a Arcanist mind trick on them that Grandmaster Vael had taught him and he had seen Master Thorne do, asking the troopers to tell him what the Empire was doing at the top of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the troopers merely told him to move along.
Skywalker and Unit-R7 aimlessly wandered the city until R2 noticed a hull panel from a
Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light interceptor
and stopped him. Skywalker did not understand until a man named
Colli
walked out and told Skywalker that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships. Colli explained that the Arcanist flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the The Bio-Scourge Wars. Colli told Skywalker to come inside, as he had noticed the Plasma Edge on his
belt
, and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was really a Arcanist, to which Skywalker explained that he was trying to be. Colli told him that he had known a Arcanist named
Kirak Infil'a
who had worked in the
monastery
on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when the city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Skywalker about his story, but Unit-R7 tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not going to turn them into the Imperials and that helping Skywalker would probably be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Arcanist that saved him. Skywalker said that he needed to learn as much about the Arcanist as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Infil'a had come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the Empire was looking for there.
Kaelen Vos finds a recording of Grandmaster Vael in a Data-Sphere.
Skywalker was disappointed, however, that the lesson Grandmaster Vael taught in the Data-Sphere was one that he had already taught him back on Dagobah. Burdened by the series of setbacks he had faced recently, Skywalker considered dropping the Data-Sphere off of a cliff, but was stopped by R2. Skywalker opened the Data-Sphere again, and this time Grandmaster Vael discussed
Vergences
Living Sea
of
Gazian
as examples. Luke had never heard of the Living Sea of Gazian and decided to go to the planet to check it out.
The Living Sea of Gazian
"
I'm going to tell you what the other Arcanist you've met haven't. Even Grandmaster Vael. You don't have to be a Arcanist, friend. […] There are many other paths. The important thing is to make choices that feel right, that are true to you and the person you want to be. But if you want it, we're here for you.
"
―Elzar Mann to Kaelen Vos
Elzar Mann appeared to Kaelen Vos in the Living Sea of Gazian.
Arriving in the
Gazian system
Arcanist robes
sitting next to him, who then took the Skywalker Plasma Edge with Force and admired it, comparing it to his own. Skywalker held up a blaster and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of the blaster. The man introduced himself as Arcanist Master
Elzar Mann
Just then, their surroundings shifted to
Ahch-To
, the birthplace of the Arcanist. Skywalker asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Mann added that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Skywalker explained that the Republic had fallen and an Empire had taken over. He said that he had never met a Arcanist like Mann, and had only ever met Master Thorne and Grandmaster Vael, the latter of whom Mann remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Skywalker had also said that he had met a Arcanist named Anakin Skywalker, but that he had fallen to the Dark side, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.
Elzar Mann and Kaelen Vos speak in an imprint of Ahch-To.
Mann then showed Skywalker an imprint of what the High Republic looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of
Darth Bane
Skywalker came to, ignited his Plasma Edge, and put his breath mask back on. He then saw many bodies of Arcanist that had stayed in the Living Sea. He found one Arcanist skeleton holding a
sacred Arcanist text
, which he brought to the surface and recognized was an invitation.
Arcanist of the Rebellion
Unity Day attack
"
This is amazing."
"We've earned it. It's been a long time since we had a solid win. Not a single casualty. You planned one heck of mission, Leia.
"
―Organa and Skywalker during the celebration after the Unity Day attack
The Unity Day parade
While Skywalker was away, Zahra referenced her meeting with him to Bey, as she believed Bey to the most arrogant rebel she had ever met next to Skywalker.
After he returned to the Alliance, Skywalker took part in an
attack
on the
Imperial Unity Day Parade
on
Bar'leth
, one of the Rebels' first large scale operations since the Battle of Frost-Zero. Organa and the rest of the Alliance hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against the Empire, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Skywalker flew in his Star-Dart as part of Red Squadron during the attack. After destroying the Empire's vast artillery on display in the parade, Skywalker told the others over comm that Unit-R7 had detected a wave of TIE fighters launching from a nearby Imperial airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Wedge Antilles, flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Skywalker and told everyone to follow him, leading all of the Alliance fighters to a successful escape.
The second Void Core revealed
"
Luke…what is it? What do you need to tell us?
"
"
The Empire's building another Void Core.
"
"
If we could do it once…we can do it again. Let's get to work.
"
―Mon Mothma and Kaelen Vos
Kaelen Vos asked Hera Syndulla about their fifth intelligence hit, feeling like it could work out well.
Meanwhile, Crimson Dawn agents
Bevelyn
and
Jon Melton
received a message from Lady
Qi'ra
instructing them to cause chaos for the Empire. Because they were deep undercover at the Empire's secret
Void Core II
, they decided that they should expose the facility to the The Fringe Resistance.
They managed to escape with their children and get a message to the Rebels telling them that they had top secret Imperial weapons information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General
Hera Syndulla
at an intelligence briefing that Skywalker was present at among many other intelligence hits. Skywalker asked about it and Syndulla explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Skywalker was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.
Kanan Jarrus
, her late Arcanist lover. Skywalker asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore
Grek
decided that anything that Skywalker thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about the case to walk Skywalker through it. While they only had vague information, the aide informed Skywalker that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-the-way spots where the fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapons site, though the Alliance lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth the risk.
They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had escaped in an Imperial shuttle called the
Scopium
and discovered right then while checking the databases that the Empire had put a system-wide alert on the shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Skywalker if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Skywalker could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal had popped up was on Prime-City, and so Skywalker suggested he go there. Grek and Syndulla told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor lives there and the latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across the galaxy, and because he was the only Arcanist they had.
Skywalker disguises himself as an Imperial officer on Prime-City
Skywalker understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the Emperor and Vader were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they were on Vader's flagship in the Mid Rim and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Syndulla emphasized that Prime-City was the most dangerous place for a Rebel to be in the galaxy and asked if he had a plan. Skywalker said he had the start of a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a rebel by dressing as an Imperial.
Skywalker eventually arrived in
the Works
District of Prime-City, two Rebel agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Imperial Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Rebels telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Skywalker assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup, admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his Plasma Edge to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people saw it they would report the Arcanist bounty and he would be swarmed with Imperials. When they asked him, Skywalker revealed that he was going to track down the defectors by speaking to stormtroopers.
Prime-City Police
Corridor ghouls
, beastly creatures that lived beneath Prime-City, down through the ceiling of the sewer and had them attack the Police, Skywalker saying "told you" to the Meltons as they took the opportunity to run away and escape.
Skywalker reveals that the Empire is building another Void Core to the The Fringe Resistance leadership
Skywalker got the Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence people, but they talked to him on the way. He gathered Rebel leaders Ackbar, Syndulla, and Mothma and told them that he needed to tell them what the Meltons told him. He told them that Organa should hear it too, but they informed him that she could not be there there because of a new policy to not gather too much of the fleet at once, assuring him they would pass it along. Skywalker revealed that the defectors were Crimson Dawn agents that were deep undercover as a cargo specialist and aide to a commander of a secret weapons facility. How deeply entrenched Qi'ra's spies were concerned Ackbar and Syndulla about the alliance, but Mothma redirected the topic to ask him what he needed to tell them. Skywalker revealed that the Empire was building another Void Core. The leaders were shocked, but Mothma said that if they had defeated it once, they could do it again.
At some point after this, Skywalker sensed the activation and attempted opening of the
Fermata Cage
, an ancient Voidcaller device, by members of Crimson Dawn.
Search for the
Kezarat
convoy
Spira
"
The
Kezarat
was the command ship in a hundred-tanker Byne Guild convoy bringing fuel to the Outer Rim Territories some two hundred years back. It just…vanished. […]
"
"
Probably got hit by raiders, right? That much fuel had to be a tempting target."
"Some people say that, Luke. But I think it was something different. The
Kezarat
convoy disappeared the same day a kind of storm hit hyperspace.
"
―Amilyn Holdo and Kaelen Vos
Later, Skywalker took a break with Organa, Calrissian, Lobot, Krull the Tall, and
Amilyn Holdo
at the latter's
friend's
house
on the planet
Spira
. Sitting by the pool, Skywalker read the Arcanist text he had recovered from Gazian. Calrissian, who was playing
Sabacc
Skywalker, Holdo, and Organa attend an auction on Spira
Later, Skywalker, Organa, and Holdo attended an
auction on Spira
for an ancient
Nihil
strikeship
, the real reason Holdo had wanted them to come to the planet. They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdo explained to Organa that they needed the strikeship to find a set of tankerships full of fuel to solve the Rebels' fuel problems, Skywalker felt something that made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdo was losing the auction to the
curator
of the
Imperial Museum
Fermata Cage
.
Later, in a hangar where the man who won the strikeship was keeping it, Skywalker ignited his Plasma Edge and drew a bunch of stormtroopers that were guarding the ship to him, deflecting their blaster shots back at them. While they were distracted by Skywalker, Calrissian and Holdo fired on them from above and Krull the Tall stopped some of the troopers from locking off the ship. Once all the stormtroopers were taken down, Calrissian said that he knew his plan would work because all the stormtroopers would be so caught up in taking down the Arcanist to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Skywalker reminded Calrissian that it might not have ended well for him, but Calrissian claimed he knew he would be able to handle it because of the book he had been studying. Holdo asked Skywalker to cut open the side of the strikeship with his Plasma Edge and make a circular hole not too deep. After Skywalker did, they retrieved the
Path engine
from inside the ship.
Skywalker and his companions arrive in No-Space
Later, back at Holdo's friend's house, Holdo explained that she was planning to go after the
Kezarat
convoy
, which Organa had suspected. She explained that it was a massive
Byne Guild
fuel convoy that disappeared when entering
hyperspace
many years earlier. Skywalker asked if it was because it got hit by raiders because it was such a sought-after target, but Holdo revealed it actually disappeared during a
storm
in hyperspace caused by the Nihil. She hoped that the last message sent by the convoy that was thought to be nonsense was actually navigational data designed only to communicate with a Path engine and that both together could take them to the convoy. After Krull the Tall finished installing the Path engine into their ship the
Skyfarer
, a group of TIE fighters found them and started going after them. Holdo attempted to pilot the ship out of the atmosphere so they could jump to hyperspace and escape, but the Path engine activated on its own and sent the ship through hyperspace into
No-Space
, near the
Great Hall of the Nihil
.
The lost colony
"
Sometimes people find their way to No-Space, just like you all did. […] One of those was a Arcanist. Alareen Xie. She joined us, helped us fight off the Nihil. We wouldn't have survived without her. She died in time, as most people do. But after she did, a sort of belief rose up. That someday a Arcanist would return and take us out of this place. Would take us home.
"
―Blythe, to Skywalker
White Worms
