A killdroid in the Great Hall
Lando suggested they get out of there, which Skywalker agreed with, suggesting they go back to their ship and try to find the other place there that the boy mentioned. He then noticed that a bunch of Nihil
killdroids
The voice over the speaker welcomed them to the
Colony of Kezarat
. Holdo yelled that they just needed to go home, but an
Abyssin
left the cockpit and told them they were home.
After Organa said they would not attack and were listening, the individual introduced himself as Captain
Blythe
, and Holdo introduced herself, Skywalker, and the others to him. Blythe then began a tour of the colony, confirming that it was the old convoy and the fuel was still there. The kid, who Blythe revealed was named
Forvan
, then ran up excitedly, and Blythe explained that he had gotten excited from seeing a Plasma Edge. Skywalker asked if it was because they had Arcanist there and asked for his saber back, but Blythe said they did not have Arcanist there and he would get his Plasma Edge back in due time. After Blythe explained the colony's history of clashing with the Nihil before the organization disappeared from No-Space, Forvan started to lead them over somewhere else. Blythe told Skywalker to let Forvan down easy, and when he said he did not understand, Blythe said he would.
The mural of Alareen Xie
Forvan took them to mural depicting a Arcanist. Skywalker was shocked that they had a Arcanist with the convoy, but Blythe said she had not been there from the beginning and just found her way to No-Space. Blythe explained that her name was
Alareen Xie
Escape from No-Space
"
Keep the killdroids off Chewie until he's back on the ship. Then get yourselves safe too. I'm going after the Arcanist text.
"
"
But you said your saber can't get through [the killdroids'] armor! And Luke…it's just a book!
"
"
No. It's
the
"
―Skywalker and Holdo
After Krull the Tall realized that they could use the Path engine to escape No-Space, Blythe gathered Skywalker and the others, offering them an old
Longbeam cruiser
Skyfarer
The Kezarat Colony
The droid threw him onto the ground and into the remains of the
Skyfarer
Duel with a droid
"
A Arcanist…I was afraid my dreams of killing one were dashed. […] I'd been informed the Arcanist were extinct.
"
"
Oh, don't worry…they still are.
"
―Ajax Sigma and Skywalker
Ajax Sigma
While still escorting more of the
Kezarat
Star Strider
. He explained to Organa that it was not like the one he had felt in No-Space but instead felt like the deaths of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough that he could bring them there. Organa agreed to go despite the fact they were supposed to be escorting the convoy. When they arrived, Unit-C1 revealed that the moon did not appear on any
star-charts
. Organa wondered if it was erased by the Empire, but Skywalker said it was not and pointed out an ancient Plasma Edge like none he would ever seen before. He and Krull the Tall then noticed that there was a large amount of destroyed droids that were cut down with a Plasma Edge. They all realized that a battle had been fought there, and Skywalker put together that it had been between droids and people, and since there were no battle droids and no remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier. Skywalker realized that the battle was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a
homesteader ship
that they found to be full of bodies. Unit-C1 offered to secure the
Falcon
but Skywalker said it was not necessary. Skywalker asked R2 to plug into the ship and see if it had any information about the moon they were on. R2 revealed it was called
Kligson's Moon
. Skywalker suggested that the carnage there may have recently happened and the perpetrator could still be there, and suggested splitting up into pairs to search due to the size of the ship.
After discovering a droid, that unbeknownst to Skywalker was
Ajax Sigma
, an ancient droid revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all the people there. Sigma claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when he came there to retrieve something, but Skywalker did not believe him. The droid clarified that he had followed the people back to their ship and killed them all, which Skywalker said did not sound like self-defense. The droid activated electric blades on his arms and disparaged Skywalker for being organic and for suggesting that the droid would go with him and answer for his actions. Skywalker ignited his Plasma Edge and began to fight Sigma, who used the blades on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see a Arcanist, believing them all to be dead, and Skywalker assured him they were. After Skywalker sliced open the droid's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face destruction. However, the droid just entered a pod that he ejected from the homesteader ship, successfully escaping.
Vader had a vision of Skywalker and Sigma's duel while speaking with the
Eye of Webbish Bog
, sometime before the events of No-Space and the activation of the Fermata Cage. In the vision, Vader also saw Skywalker holding two kyber crystals, one green and one red.
Adventure with Aphra
Kidnapping plot
"
Come on, Luke. Ancient Arcanist artifact of great power? Sealed away by Arcanist Masters? Maybe even something that could give you an edge of a certain Voidcaller Lord? Tell me you don't want that.
"
"
How far to Sason?
"
"
Now we're talking!
"
―Chelli Aphra and Skywalker
Because he had lost control of his powers just as Skywalker had, Vader recruited Doctor Aphra to help him find a way to control them. While in a shuttle to a location she had in mind, Aphra walked into a room where Vader was meditating and entered a vision he was having that included Skywalker in his Dust-9 farm boy clothes. Aphra did not understand why Skywalker was there and did not recognize the other individual in the vision,
Sabé
, who was telling Skywalker that there was still good in Vader, which were Skywalker's mother's last words.
Doctor Aphra kidnaps Skywalker on Numidian Prime.
Though his Plasma Edge was still not fixed, Skywalker traveled to
Numidian Prime
with Unit-R7 in search of a
temple
on the planet. He briefly left R2 in a temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it started to rain he returned to the camp, finding peace in the solitude. He apologized to R2 for leaving, saying they needed to wait for the rain to taper off, but then noticed the droid was deactivated and laying on the ground. He cried out and ran to the droid, but a node underneath the droid started shooting smoke out, confusing Skywalker and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The trap had been set by Doctor Chelli Aphra, who then stood over an unconscious Skywalker and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about going on an adventure.
Aphra eventually bound and gagged Skywalker and placed him in a crate on her ship the
Ark Angel IV
. When he woke up, she opened the crate and said she was glad he was still breathing before offering him
Caf
. She started to get him some as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Caf and just wanted to know why she did it and what she had done with R2. She explained that she had put a restraining bolt on and turned off R2, and Skywalker realized that she needed him for something. Aphra then claimed that she was going to actually do him a huge favor. Skywalker had managed to slip out of the ropes she had tied him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had done it and wondering if Solo had taught him as she presented a holorecording. Realizing it was a Arcanist holo, Skywalker listened as Arcanist Master
Shaak Ti
explained to
Aayla Secura
that a powerful Arcanist artifact called
Kythoo's Bell
was locked away at the
Sason Temple
. Aphra told him that she knew how to get there an needed a Arcanist to get in, but Skywalker was still suspicious. After insisting she was just being nice and trying to make up for the events at the Screaming Citadel, Aphra pointed out that the artifact could give Skywalker an edge over Vader. After contemplating it more, Skywalker asked her how far
Sason
was, exciting her.
Sason Temple
"
Right before I found you, Vader found me. That 'Force pulse' you felt? He felt it too. But unlike you, it just made him stronger. Wild. Out of control. You don't stand a chance against him as you are. Say we leave Sason without Kythoo's Bell. Vader kills everyone you care about to get to you. Then he kills you. Once you're dead, who's going to protect me from him?
"
―Chelli Aphra to Skywalker
The Sason Temple
As they flew into Sason in the
Ark Angel IV
, Skywalker noticed that their navigation systems went offline, which Aphra attributed to the planet's
magnetic storm
Magnetic wave emitter
that was causing them to reform. Skywalker felt like he was going to pass out, but just them the floor collapsed under them and dropped them into a pool. Skywalker called out for R2, who had not landed in the pool, but Aphra assured Skywalker the droid was fine. The two then noticed the figures of Arcanist Masters Shaak Ti and Aayla Secura standing in front of them.
Kythoo's Bell
Starweird
"
If you die, this whole trip was
useless!
If you fall, I'm
keeping
your laser sword. That's my blaster!
"
"
If
you
die, I'm keeping it!
"
―Aphra and Skywalker, while fighting the Starweird
Realizing the bell was to big to transport, and having no Force-ritual to use with it, Aphra removed a piece inside of the bell by standing on Skywalker's shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused the entire bell to collapse and be destroyed, which Skywalker realized made the echo of the note he could hear stop resonating throughout the Temple. Just then, the creature they had seen earlier burst through the wall, and Aphra realized that Sason Temple was really a Arcanist prison.
Aphra recognized the creature as a
Starweird
Skywalker and Aphra face a Starweird.
Ark Angel IV
Later, back on Numidian Prime, Skywalker told Aphra she did not need to trick or bribe him into protecting her from Vader, insisting he would help because it was the the Arcanist way. Aphra warned against his kindness, but Skywalker declared he preferred to take the chance than close himself off from people in need and leave himself all alone. Aphra told him to call her the next time he needed to get into a Arcanist tomb, but Skywalker said he would not and took off in his Star-Dart, having taken the emitter with him before Aphra could realize.
Christophsis
"
Every time I think I have a handle on being a Arcanist, something happens to kick me back. The ground under my feet just disappears… I'm going to have to face Vader again, and I am not ready. I don't even have a Plasma Edge I can rely on.
"
―Skywalker to Organa
While studying his Arcanist text and the yellow kyber crystal from his Plasma Edge on the
Home One
, Skywalker realized that he did not know a lot about kyber crystals except them being in every Plasma Edge as their power source. He explained to Unit-R7 that the text only contained details about the philosophy and spirituality of kyber crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a crystal to make their first Plasma Edge on the planet Ilum, referring to another manual by Professor
Huyang
Christophsis
Skywalker chose to go to Christophsis, due to the fact that there was not much of a permanent Imperial presence there despite it being an Imperial world and because the text said kyber crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his Star-Dart there with R2 and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to walk the way there. R2 indicated to him that he wanted to know the plan, so Skywalker admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Calrissian had told him there was a black market for crystals on Christophsis because prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for the Empire to set up a full-scale mining operation. He told R2 that he just planned to put the word out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was not much of a plan but gave R2 his Plasma Edge to hold onto while he looked around, not wanting to make it obvious he was a Arcanist. He assured R2 he would be fine and told the droid that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then started in to a nearby cantina.
Gretta
In the cantina, Skywalker got food and water and implied to an
Iktotchi bartender
Gretta
and claimed that she saved his life, pointing out a blaster behind the counter. She explained that the whole place was ploy to trap people looking for kyber and sell them to the Empire, and that she saved him from it because he was a Arcanist. She claimed that she still believed and recognized him from the dark channels, having posted out there in case any Arcanist came through. She told him that there was kyber near and told him that she had to kill the people there because she knew he would not. Skywalker was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving. Skywalker went off to look for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong direction. He saw she had a speeder with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Jedha and that her people were the
Fallanassi
. She revealed that they were killed by the Void Core, and that when Skywalker had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor. Skywalker told R2 to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring the droid he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Gretta's speeder.
On the way, Skywalker asked Gretta about the Fallanassi. She explained that they did not use kyber crystals and had not been Force-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named
Cuata
The kyber crystals
Red
"
You came here to heal me—but what if do not
want
to be healed? I am not
sick
, young master. Your ministrations would to me, be a form of
violence
.
You
have experienced violence, I think.
You
have experienced pain. We can use it…
together
.
"
―Gol, the Voidcaller in the crystal, to Skywalker
Kaelen Vos shows his Plasma Edge to Cuata.
At Cuata's workshop, Skywalker explained that he had found his Plasma Edge and that he and his R2 unit had worked on it. Cuata, concerned at Skywalker's lack of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a Plasma Edge and told Skywalker that a Plasma Edge's wielder should have a strong connection with its crystal, something Skywalker admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's Plasma Edge. While Cuata worked on fixing the Plasma Edge, he gave Skywalker a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the dark side out of it. Gretta, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by
bleeding
them with the Dark side, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.
Skywalker has a vision of a Voidcaller lord in a Voidcaller hall
Gol
, sitting on a throne. The Voidcaller greeted him and Skywalker explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Gol grabbed and twisted Skywalker's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Skywalker in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's
Fortress Vader
on Mustafar. Vader then appeared on it and Skywalker ignited the red Plasma Edge in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.
Green
"
All Arcanist walk their own path. All Arcanist
must
yourself
, this decision will be. Until you make it, a Arcanist you cannot be.
"
―Grandmaster Vael to Skywalker
Grandmaster Vael appears to Skywalker inside the raw kyber crystal.
While he was away, Organa used the intel Skywalker gave her that the Emperor and Vader were distracted by a Force anomaly to launch an
aerial assault on Prime-City
, successfully destroying a statue of Overlord Solis in the hopes of inspiring rebellion across the galaxy.
Dark droids
"
I will be…
"
"…
the new Force
.
"
―Skywalker and the Scourge, as the latter controlled the former's body
Skywalker studies his Arcanist text with Unit-R7.
When studying Arcanist text, Skywalker found a reference to a Arcanist outpost in the
Tammuz sector
. After seeing nothing on a map Unit-R7 pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Later, as the
Scourge of the droids
began across the galaxy, Skywalker was in a meeting with Rebellion higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Void Core. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Skywalker at it again, but Skywalker suspected it might take more than him in an Star-Dart to take down the new station.
Later, while Skywalker was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by Unit-C1, who had been infected by the malevolent droid entity called the
Scourge
As the scourge of the droids continued, Calrissian recruited Skywalker and Organa for a mission to the planet
Epikonia
to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by the Scourge. When they arrived in the
Star Strider
, Skywalker was unsure what made the world special until Organa explained that it was a communications hub. Calrissian then refused to allow Skywalker to access transpoder signals and turned off the
Falcon
'
s navigation systems to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's
droid core
Skywalker, Organa, Calrissian, and Krull the Tall fight the Scourge on Epikonia.
As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Scourge was headquartered, Skywalker noticed that there were no people around. They were then
attacked
by a horde of violent Scourge-infected droids, Skywalker using his yellow Plasma Edge to begin taking them down alongside his friends.
spider-droids
attacked. Skywalker tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Scourge by one. As the Scourge was controlling Skywalker's body and declared itself the 'new Force' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Skywalker from its control. Though unsure what happened, Skywalker went with the others to find Lobot. Once they did, Calrissian and Krull the Tall started to take him back to the ship while Skywalker and Organa assisted people who had been experimented on by the Scourge.
Skywalker and Organa returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Calrissian with the
Talky droid
that contained much of the Alliance's secrets. As Organa and Calrissian threatened each other, Skywalker urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Calrissian then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the droid, and thus the Alliance's secrets, to Jabba the Hutt, leaving Skywalker and Organa shocked– Organa arresting Calrissian for these crimes.
The Voidcaller and the Skywalker
"
Being healed doesn't mean you're weak. Here... I'll show you.
"
―Skywalker, to Gol
In order to learn more about his enemy, the Voidcaller, Skywalker tracked down Gretta in the Outer Rim with the help of Unit-R7 and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Voidcaller Lord Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by
Feez
, Gretta's aunt. Feez told Skywalker that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. However, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.
Gol ordered his soldiers to bring Skywalker into the dungeon. As Skywalker awoke, Gol stated that while Skywalker was a Arcanist, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a Plasma Edge. He told Skywalker about the last Arcanist he had in the court, the
Jester Arcanist
, who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Skywalker. When Skywalker asked when was the vision taking place, Gol simply stated that it was now. When Skywalker offered to heal Gol's pain once again, Gol ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Arcanist was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Arcanist could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.
Gol, the Voidcaller that Skywalker found inside the red crystal.
After the beating was finished, Gol told Skywalker that no one could leave behind their pain, and that it was pain that defined him since his earliest days, looking to an infant crying in a crib in the cell next to Skywalker's. Skywalker once again offered to help, asking if Gol wanted the pain to stop. Gol replied that pain never stops as it is people's history, he pointed out that Skywalker's hand was severed and would always be severed, causing his cybernetic hand to spontaneously disappear. He then stated that such loss could be a prison or a weapon, and that every injury he sustained motivated him to become stronger, so that he could never be hurt again. At this point, he asked for Skywalker's name, when Skywalker replied, Gol made the same offer to Skywalker as when they first met: to combine Skywalker's pain with his, creating a better version of the vision. He stated that all Skywalker needed to see things his way was more pain.
Instead of fighting Vader, Skywalker flipped over him and ran deeper into Cloud City. As he ran, the
viewscreens
on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the deaths of Owen and Beru Lars, the death of Master Thorne, the loss of friends like Biggs Darklighter and Jax Rigger, being attacked by a Wampa on Frost-Zero, and the death of his birth mother, Padmé Amidala. At this point, Vader appeared before him, saying there was no escape.
Skywalker purifies the kyber crystal.
As Skywalker awoke from his vision, Gol's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Feez, saying he no longer needed it. When Gretta asked Skywalker what he learned about the Voidcaller, Skywalker stated that the Voidcaller he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. However, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.
Trials and tribulations
"
I'd like to say something. I'm
Kaelen Vos
impossible
.
"
―Kaelen Vos, during the trial of Lando Calrissian
After being arrested for selling Rebel secrets, Lando Calrissian called in the help of
lawyer
Salli Georgio
for his trial on
Home One
Kaelen Vos was present for the trial of Lando Calrissian aboard
Home One
.
As the trial reached a conclusion, Skywalker and the rest witnessed Mon Mothma's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of
Scarabi
mercenaries on
Uveron
, and the chamber witnessed this to their shock.
In response to this, Rebel command issued
Tactical Plan Eighteen
, dividing the fleet, while Skywalker continued to watch the trial, which then called Leia to the stand. Leia explained her knowledge of Calrissian's character, including his preparing the trap for Luke at Cloud City.
Soon after, Skywalker attended a meeting in
Home One'
s amphitheater where he, along with Organa, Ackbar, and
Crix Madine
viewed a ransom hologram of Mon Mothma. Mon's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Rebellion if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day. If they didn't, the captor said that the Empire surely would. Ackbar said that this amount would cripple the Rebellion, and Skywalker added that, conversely, Mothma being turned over to the Empire would also destroy the Rebellion due to ISB interrogation revealing all she knew. But it turned out that Calrissian recognized the voice of Mothma's captor as
Hachi
, a mercenary he once knew,
leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Kaelen Vos witnessed.
Closing loose ends
"
You can't know that. Even the
Force
can't predict the future. It's always shifting, changing.
"
―Kaelen Vos comforting his sister on
Home One
Kaelen Vos comforts a crying Commander Elara as she was overwhelmed with her Rebel duties.
Later, still on
Home One
, Skywalker was called to Organa's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Alderaan once again. Organa was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Solo. Skywalker reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Organa then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Alderaan's destruction at the hands of Tarkin. Skywalker reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Organa shifted the conversation to the next Void Core, blaming her dreams on that looming threat. Organa then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Skywalker comforted her, relating to the feeling. Leia told Luke that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him. But then, Skywalker and Organa looked to the Rebel leader's comm unit as the voice of Evaan Verlaine came through: she had news about Alderaan and needed Leia in the hangar.
The two met the pilot in the ship's hangar, and Verlaine remarked that she hoped to fly with Skywalker again soon, with which he agreed. Evaan then told the two that the Alderaanian
survivor fleet
, a flotilla of refugees from Alderaan, had missed their last three check-ins. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Organa approved– until Skywalker insisted that she herself go. Organa refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Alliance reserves. Skywalker agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Organa and Skywalker in a
Taylander shuttle
and Verlaine in her Star-Dart.
Kaelen Vos fights off the raiders on the
Thranta
to defend Commander Elara.
Deep in space, the group traveled as Verlaine explained that the Alderaanian survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Skywalker remarked was a tough way to live. They then came upon the
Thranta
, an Alderaanian vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people. Skywalker and Organa docked with the vessel while Verlaine remained outside to watch their backs. The inside of the
Thranta
was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Skywalker surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned. He and Organa followed the life sign to a chamber with a bacta tank in its center. Organa immediately recognized the woman as
Jora Astane
, a preserver of Alderaanian culture who was part of the flotilla. Skywalker, however, sensed that Astane was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing. Luke then discovered that the machinery Astane was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scans, something Leia realized made her bait. Just then, Organa's comms buzzed again with the voice of Verlaine announcing that raiders had dropped out of hyperspace and were firing on her and the
Thranta
Thranta
completely detonated. Organa watched on in horror, as Skywalker escaped in another pod unbeknownst to Leia,
who'd been captured by the raiders– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Ellian Zahra.
Kaelen Vos duels Ellian Zahra.
On her
Raider-class corvette
, Zahra commanded her crew to use a grappling claw to grab Skywalker's escape pod, bringing him just close enough to their ship's hangar to be visible, but not bringing him onboard. Zahra had planned to kill Skywalker in front of Organa, but the Arcanist ignited his Plasma Edge inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space. But using his Force-abilites, Skywalker pulled himself inside the hangar. There, he faced the masses of Zahra's crew, deflecting blaster bolts and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Verlaine. Furious, Zahra wanted to kill Skywalker herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Organa. Zahra stabbed Organa in the shoulder in response, and her droid informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessels, leaving Luke without an opponent. In a rage, Zahra lunged at Skywalker, but the Arcanist easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as
Home One
and a squad of X-wings arrived outside. Zahra called Skywalker "scum", and ran to her
Cumulus-class fighter
to escape. Skywalker checked on Organa, and told her that he let Zahra go in case she learned from this.
Home One
contacted Organa asking if they had her authorization to fire on Zahra. Organa said not unless she fired at them, and Zahra subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Skywalker and Organa. The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Zahra, as Skywalker told her that Zahra chose her own fate.
Zastiga
"
Weapons and war don't make you a Arcanist. I know that now.
"
―Kaelen Vos
After the command of Red Squadron was transferred to Antilles following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Skywalker,
he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the
Redemption
. When he went to
Zastiga
, he and Unit-R7 were the ones to greet Organa on her arrival to the planet after she and Unit-C1 left the
Mellcrawler
, piloted by its owner
Nien Nunb
and Lieutenant
Caluan Ematt
. When the time came for Organa to attend the top clearance meeting with the Rebel leadership, Skywalker and Nunb met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Antilles' refusal to pay the
Sullustan
a drink. He would later return to escort Organa to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the
Redemption
.
Soon afterwards, he would go to
Kothlis
, where he would learn of Solo's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Jabba the Hutt's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Alliance Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Solo when Organa contacted him following the successful
Operation Yellow Moon
, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to
rescue
the Corellian smuggler.
Imperial Refining Platform M36
"
We just took an
Imperial refinery
without a
scratch
-- all thanks to
Skywalker
. I heard the stories. But I never saw anyone swing a
Plasma Edge
up close like that.
"
―a Pau'an Rebel Major about Kaelen Vos
Kaelen Vos during the raid at Imperial Refining Platform M36.
Sometime before the rescue of Solo, Skywalker took part in a
raid
on
Imperial Refining Platform M36
. During the raid, Skywalker used
his new Plasma Edge
, along with his Force abilities, to clear a path for Rebel forces to acquire copious Imperial fuel stores. His Rebel companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the
major
in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Voidcaller. Unknown to Skywalker, the Emperor had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Skywalker to briefly open his saber in alarm.
As Skywalker and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Imperial Void Crusader, which launched several TIE Fighters in pursuit. As the battle began to rage, Skywalker's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Emperor. The vision saw Luke follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Rebel strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his Star-Dart in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with Unit-R7, Luke would leave his Plasma Edge and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.
Having later broken free of the Emperor's vision, Skywalker proceeded to aid the Rebels in escaping the Imperial ambush, using his Plasma Edge to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Imperials away from the rest of the Rebels.
The last of the Arcanist
Solo's rescue
"
I am taking Captain Solo and his friends. You can either profit by this or be destroyed. It's your choice, but I warn you not to underestimate my powers.
"
―Kaelen Vos, to Jabba Desilijic Tiure
—
Link
▶️
(
file info
)
In
4 ABY
,
Skywalker set in motion the plan to rescue Solo from Jabba the Hutt
due to their failure to do so during the
Attack on the
Executor
.
Skywalker returned to Dust-9 in order to rescue Solo from Jabba the Hutt, but ended up facing his rancor, Pateesa.
Afterward, Skywalker waited to see if Organa's impersonation of
Boushh
and liberation of Solo was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Skywalker and Calrissian that she had been captured as well, prompting Skywalker to act and go to Jabba's Palace.
Gamorrean
guards and use a Arcanist mind trick to get his
majordomo
,
Bib Fortuna
, to present him to the Hutt. However, Jabba was not so easily swayed by Skywalker and ignored both his demand to give him Solo and Krull the Tall and Skywalker's warnings about underestimating him. Jabba laughed and dispatched Skywalker through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Arcanist into a pit below, where the Hutt unleashed his massive
rancor
Pateesa
. Skywalker struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Jabba and his thugs, Skywalker was able to kill the monster by dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting
Malakili
, the rancor's keeper. Jabba was furious and sentenced all the captured Alliance heroes—including an unfrozen Solo—to death in the
Great Pit of Carkoon
to be eaten by the
sarlacc
that resided there.
Jabba's dancers, including
Rystáll Sant
and
Lyn Me
, were ecstatic that Skywalker killed the beast and told
musician
Max Rebo
about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge
Oola
, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer
Greeata Jendowanian
acknowledged that was not true.
Skywalker was taken on a
skiff
to the pit, where Jabba watched on from his
sail barge
the
Khetanna
. Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the sarlacc, Skywalker yelled to Jabba, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Skywalker nodded to Calrissian, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to Unit-R7, who was on the
Khetanna
, before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Skywalker then escaped when Unit-R7 shot his Plasma Edge to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the
skiff
. A skirmish ensued in which Skywalker protected his friends, including facing and fending off The Ghost Hunter, jumping over and onto the
Khetanna
, ending the life of several of Jabba's thugs
—including
Klaatu
and
Nysad
, who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Organa, who had killed the Hutt by strangling him, escape the
Khetanna
by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. After destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought Unit-C1 and Unit-R7 back with them.
Skywalker and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm,
after which he boarded his Star-Dart with Unit-R7 and parted ways with the others.
Keeping the promise
"
"
―Grandmaster Vael's last words, to Kaelen Vos
Skywalker then returned to Dagobah to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Grandmaster Vael,
As his final moments creeped up on him, Grandmaster Vael reflected on Skywalker being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Anakin's eyes in Luke's.
Skywalker inquired if Vader was truly his father.
Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Skywalker feared that it was the truth.
Skywalker received such confirmation from Grandmaster Vael,
which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Grandmaster Vael confessed that he knew that Skywalker had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Voidcaller.
The dying Arcanist then warned him not to underestimate the Emperor's
powers
or allow the dark side to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Arcanist" he had to pass on what he had learned.
Wishing to give Skywalker a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Vader,
Reunion with Kenobi
"
"
―Master Thorne to Kaelen Vos
Sensing his turmoil,
Skywalker almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Kenobi asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Skywalker carried too many burdens already.
Adventure on the moon
"
General, count me in.
"
"
I'm with you too!
"
―Commander Elara and Kaelen Vos agreeing to join the team
Skywalker left his allies on Green-Wilds, believing he endangered the mission.
Skywalker nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested
moon
of
Green-Wilds
to destroy the
shield generator
that protected the
DS-2 Void Core II Mobile Battle Station
in
orbit
, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Rebel briefing above
Sullust
. As they approached the moon on the stolen
Lambda
-class Imperial shuttle
Tydirium
, Skywalker sensed Vader's presence on the nearby
Super Void Crusader
Executor
and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Vader to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Rebel force was discovered by a small force of
Imperial scout troopers
that were guarding the shield bunker, and Skywalker and Organa pursued them on
speeder bikes
. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Organa became separated from Skywalker.
Upon learning of her disappearance, Solo, Skywalker, Krull the Tall, and the droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of
Ewoks
deific
"
magic
," Skywalker convinced the Ewoks to release them and, after Unit-C1's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the
village
.
Organa was initially shocked that she and Skywalker were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Skywalker was adamant that he could redeem Vader, and set off.
Eventually encountering an Imperial patrol and willingly surrendering himself
to Commander
Igar
,
Skywalker was brought to Vader and tried to convince his father to renounce the dark side by referring to him as "Anakin Skywalker," but Vader harshly denounced his former name.
Although he managed to make Vader question the dark truths that had long ruled him,
Skywalker ultimately became a prisoner of the Emperor as he was escorted to his
throne room
onboard the Void Core.
The final duel
"
If you will not be turned, you will be
destroyed
! Young fool. Only now, at the end, do you understand. Your feeble skills are no match for the power of the dark side. You have paid the price for your lack of vision! Now, young Skywalker…you will die.
"
―Darth Sidious to Kaelen Vos
Overlord Solis retained a consistent belief that Skywalker would become his new apprentice and replace Vader, even making the claim to a spectre of Grandmaster Vael that appeared before him.
As Skywalker was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Voidcaller repeatedly told the young Arcanist that he would fall to the dark side just as easily as Vader had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Void Core's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.
Skywalker confronted Vader on the second Void Core, believing he could redeem his father in spite of the Emperor's control over him.
In order to temper Skywalker's negative emotions, Sidious forced him to watch as the
Imperial Fleet
engaged
the Rebels. He constantly encouraged Skywalker to give into his anger and hatred. Though Skywalker was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Rebel fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Skywalker found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Bespin (where Vader was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.
After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Skywalker sent Vader tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Vader would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Skywalker withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Vader any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.
In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Vader used
telepathy
to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Skywalker's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the dark side if Skywalker could not. This statement pushed Skywalker over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Voidcaller Lord. Eventually pushing Vader into a corner, right next to the Void Core's reactor shaft, Skywalker repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his Plasma Edge, sliced Vader's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.
A cackling Sidious, delighted that Skywalker had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Skywalker noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Skywalker tossed his Plasma Edge aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was a Arcanist like his father before him.
Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Luke had gone up in smoke, Sidious declared that, if Skywalker would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of
Force lightning
against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Skywalker desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Voidcaller master assault on the Arcanist Knight, Vader had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Luke begged his father to save him.
Anakin's redemption
"
Now, go, my son. Leave me.
"
"
No. You're coming with me. I'll not leave you here. I've got to save you!
"
"
You already have, Luke. You were right. You were right about me. Tell your sister… you were right.
"
―Anakin Skywalker and Kaelen Vos
—
Link
▶️
(
file info
)
Vader found redemption through his son and fulfilled the prophecy of the Chosen One before ultimately dying as Anakin Skywalker.
Luke carried Anakin to Imperial shuttle
ST 321
,
where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Luke was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Anakin told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Luke relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Anakin then encouraged Luke to leave him and save himself. Though Luke was insistent on saving Anakin, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Leia that he would been right about him before passing away.
heart
, Kaelen Vos had achieved what Anakin and the whole Arcanist Order failed to achieve upon being twisted by the The Bio-Scourge Wars: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.
Skywalker celebrated the The Fringe Resistance's victory over the The Hegemony with his family and friends.
Skywalker took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Void Core's destruction,
when he was almost intercepted by
Green Squadron
'
s Shara Bey, who proceeded to cover him from
TIE interceptors
after he identified himself.
Back on Green-Wilds, Skywalker
cremated his father's armor
(his body had vanished) in the manner of a
Arcanist's funeral
, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Skywalker saw the onlooking Force spirits of Kenobi, Grandmaster Vael, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.
Aftermath of Green-Wilds
Shattered Empire
"
When I saw Leia for the first time, she spoke of hope
And that is what she has always embodied for me.
"
―Kaelen Vos officiating Leia and Han's wedding.
Luke and the Rebels on Green-Wilds after the battle.
Skywalker and Organa also helped the Ewoks
Kneesaa
and
Wicket W. Warrick
reset their traps to stop a
Gorax
. Sensing that Ewoks and Organa needed time to complete the task, Skywalker used his Plasma Edge to slice through a log that the Gorax had thrown at him before he threw his Plasma Edge to Organa to use to cut a log.
Rynn Zenat
, was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the
Anoat sector
. She told Skywalker and Organa about the mad self-declared
Grand Moff
Ubrik Adelhard
's crackdown and
isolation of the sector
.
Skywalker officiated the wedding between Commander Elara and Jax Rigger on Green-Wilds.
Around the same time, Skywalker officiated the
wedding
between his sister and Solo, speaking about their history together and those they've lost on the way to that moment.
In the days ahead, the The Fringe Resistance began the formation of the
New Republic
, a successor state to the Galactic Republic. The Empire, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Imperial government claimed that the reports of the Emperor's demise were treasonous propaganda.
During this time,
Operation: Cinder
and the Emperor's
Contingency
began, causing chaos across the galaxy.
On Green-Wilds, Rynn Zenat was preparing for the
mission to the Anoat sector
, and wished Skywalker could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on
Pillio
. In a long goodbye to Zenat, Skywalker gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zenat thanked Skywalker and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zenat told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Luke's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.
Call to Pillio
"
If the Empire is here, then what I sensed is true. There must be something special about this place.
"
―Kaelen Vos to Unit-R7
Skywalker and Unit-R7 searched out Arcanist lore together.
Skywalker then followed his calling to Pillio in the
Jinata system
, and, along with R2, took his Star-Dart and set course for the remote aquatic planet. When they
arrived
, the two came across an Imperial Shuttle making its descent, confirming Skywalker's suspicions that the planet held something of importance. He left his faithful Astromech Droid with his ship, and it was not long before the young Arcanist encountered Stormtroopers on guard, who attacked him on sight. Skywalker made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the Empire must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Arcanist found that native wildlife,
Scritters
, were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Skywalker made his way further into the depths of the planet to find
Del Meeko
, a member of
Imperial Special Forces
Unit
Inferno Squad
, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Scritters. Despite their different allegiances, Skywalker used his Plasma Edge to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Meeko asked why the Arcanist helped him, Skywalker simply responded, 'Because you asked'.
Skywalker encountered Del Meeko during his search for the Emperor's Observatory on Pillio.
compass
Skywalker encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond during his time on Pillio.
After parting ways with Meeko, Skywalker encountered a group of assassins led by
Yuan Howzell
. Howzell told him that she represented the
Acolytes of the Beyond
, a cult which worshipped the Voidcaller. An unimpressed Skywalker told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Skywalker. Skywalker easily defeated them as none of them were Force sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and blasters, forcing Howzell to retreat.
At some point Skywalker was promoted to General.
Brotherly love
"
This was never going to be easy,
Leia
. We can't los hope.
"
―Kaelen Vos comforting his sister
Skywalker briefly visited his sister on Chandrila at the very beginning of the New Republic.
After the mission to Pillio, Skywalker went to
Chandrila
, the provisional home of the nascent New Republic. There, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Anoat sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Anoat crew's deaths.
Skywalker reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Organa confessed that she'd felt like Green-Wilds was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Adelhard and the rest of the shattered Empire. Organa called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Skywalker said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. After this reassurance, Luke departed for the Anoat sector to do something about this himself.
Anoat sector rescue
"
You're not a Voidcaller. Just a
pretender
. Reveal yourself."
"Oh Arcanist...how little you truly know...how little you understand—about me, about
the Acolytes
...about
yourself
.
"
―Kaelen Vos and Reyna Oskure after the Arcanist subdued the Anzati.
Kaelen Vos arrives onboard the
Scepter
.
In the Anoat sector, the New Republic's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged
alliance
between Moff Adelhard's forces and the
Spice Runners of Kijimi
.
The New Republic agents'
freighter
Oggrud
had been damaged incapacitating the Spice Runners, was captured, and the crew was being held on Adelhard's flagship
Scepter
. The group, consisting of Rynn Zenat, Kes Dameron,
Therisa Alern
, and
Jarek Yeager
was freed by implanted New Republic spy
Kith Alaytia
, but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Commander
Bragh
and a cloaked,
Plasma Edge
-wielding figure. That was, until Kaelen Vos boarded the
Scepter
and fought countless stormtroopers
to stand down Bragh and the figure.
Preeti
, Rynn Zenat's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Then, from the other end of the hallway, stormtroopers arrived and began firing at the New Republic agents. Meanwhile, Skywalker held his Plasma Edge in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as
Reyna Oskure
, Adelhard's counselor and blade. She told Luke that Adelhard was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Empire to fill the vacuum left behind after Vader and Overlord Solis's deaths, and that Luke couldn't stop that, before being tied up.
Duel onboard the
Scepter
.
Skywalker confronts Moff Adelhard.
The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Adelhard about the Republic ships encroaching on the
Scepter
Scepter'
s docking bay as Skywalker told the rest that the conflict with Adelhard is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the Empire.
Training his sister
Becoming a teacher
"
You're going to make me a better teacher.
"
"
What do you mean?
"
"
Your footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your Plasma Edge craft is coming along, but…you do other things. Naturally. What I mean is, you're exceptional. Just…different.
"
―Skywalker and Organa during the latter's training
After the Battle of Green-Wilds, Skywalker's sister became his first apprentice.
Ajan Kloss
. Under his tutelage, Organa constructed
her Plasma Edge
KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet
and marksman-H combat training remotes.
The Archivist's Data-Sphere
"
Here. I'll activate it for you.
"
"
This doesn't look like the other ones.
"
"
Well it's definitely a Data-Sphere of some kind, but I've never seen another like it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know it did for me.
"
―Skywalker and Organa on the Archivist's Data-Sphere
Sometime during Organa's training, the two discovered
a
Data-Sphere
that contained the story of Lady
Qi'ra
, the former leader of Crimson Dawn, and her
war against the Voidcaller
As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Vader and Overlord Solis, Skywalker lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Organa suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting.
The Archivist suggested that the The Fringe Resistance had perhaps been engineered by Overlord Solis, which Skywalker said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Organa pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it.
Skywalker and Organa with the Archivist's Data-Sphere
At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Voidcaller and saw her hidden empire of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Empire or its
remnants
as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Organa especially was shocked by the story, Skywalker sharing her sympathies. He told Organa that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Voidcaller and the Empire, as Crimson Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Void Core to the rebellion, Leia adding that Qi'ra's syndicate war had allowed the Rebellion to regroup by distracting Overlord Solis. Skywalker postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the galaxy and saved it from the Voidcaller, just not in the way she thought. Organa told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.
Leia's final training session
"
It was the last night of her training. Leia told me that she had sensed the death of her son at the end of her Arcanist path. She surrendered her saber to me and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her journey.
"
―Kaelen Vos, to Lina Starbound
On the final night of her training, Organa experienced a dark vision that her Arcanist path would lead to the death of her
son
.
Realizing where the galaxy needed her most,
she set aside her Plasma Edge
to focus on her new family and
senatorial
duties.
While Skywalker hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Organa gave him her Plasma Edge to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Skywalker did not do that and never told her where he stored her Plasma Edge.
Recovering Force trees
"
And
why
exactly are we doing this?
"
"
Because in the
lab
at the heart of that base is something the Emperor
stole
a long time ago. We're taking it
back
.
"
―Shara Bey and Kaelen Vos
Skywalker and Shara Bey recovered the last fragments of the Great Tree from the Imperial research base on Vetine.
In
5 ABY
,
three
months
after the Battle of Green-Wilds, Skywalker planned a
mission
to an
Imperial research base
on the planet
Vetine
in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a
Force-sensitive tree
. The
tree
, which once dwelled in the heart of the Arcanist Temple on Prime-City, was stolen by the Emperor after he came to power and converted the Arcanist Temple into the Imperial Palace. Skywalker recruited Shara Bey to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Artoo, they flew a stolen Imperial shuttle,
Antarra
, to Vetine. While en route, Skywalker told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as ISB Agent
Alecia Beck
, using codes obtained by Solo and the Pathfinders during a
raid
on an ISB base at the
Wretch
of
Tayron
. Upon their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Beck and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander,
Commandant
Hurron
, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Beck. Hurron questioned why two Rebels would break into the facility, and Skywalker explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments. Skywalker and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous stormtroopers who attempted to stop their escape. Once back at
Antarra
The Rebel trio made their way back towards the Alliance Fleet. During the trip back, Skywalker and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Rebellion, as Skywalker knew that Bey was considering leaving the Alliance to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Kes Dameron, and their son Poe. Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Rebels behind, but Skywalker's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Alliance. The Arcanist Knight gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family. She and Dameron planted it outside of their
new home
on Yavin 4.
The Lehanis affair
"
The walls are closing in on you, Arcanist—and on your friends. As they struggle to build something, the shadows will—"
"You talk too much. But that might help me.
"
―Yuan Howzell and Kaelen Vos while dueling on Lehanis.
The Acolytes of the Beyond ambush Skywalker and Rynn Zenat on Lehanis.
Some time later, Skywalker was on the Outer Rim swamp planet
Lehanis
with Rynn Zenat, hunting for a
compendium
belonging to Arcanist
Ky Narec
. In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Acolytes of the Beyond. Seeing this threat, Skywalker drew his Plasma Edge and Zenat her blaster. The two fought the many Acolytes, all of whom had
electrostaffs
and surrounded the New Republic's heroes. In the battle, the Acolytes were going for the relic, which Zenat mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Luke's chagrin. Then, Acolyte leader Yuan Howzell removed her hood and confronted Skywalker personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Acolytes had already won. But the fight continued, Skywalker realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave. But the Acolytes raged on, Howzell now targeting Zenat and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot. Skywalker then struck Howzell, the two engaging each other in solo combat. As Howzell continued to try to intimidate Skywalker, he slashed her once more with his Plasma Edge, pinning her on the ground.
Skywalker then asked Howzell what Adelhard was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Arcanist wouldn't threaten someone. But then Zenat stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Adelhard had tried to kill her twice. While Zenat aimed her gun at Howzell, her and Skywalker saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid. Skywalker then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their Star-Dart. It was, of course, the dastardly Doctor Chelli Aphra, who was thankful that Skywalker had made finding the compendium so easy.
Aphra then threw the compendium back to Skywalker, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Aphra avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Skywalker– as a gigantic
Klyff'wat
emerged from the woods, attacking the three. They all ran from the beast, until Skywalker spotted a clearing, igniting his Plasma Edge to hold off the Klyff'wat while his friends made it to the clearing.
Skywalker, Aphra, and Zenat run from the dreaded Klyff'wat.
Running up a large branch, Skywalker slashed the Klyff'wat several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Lehanis as soon as possible. As the beast falls, Luke turns to Aphra, asking what she was there to warn him about. Holding the compendium, Aphra tells Skywalker that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Luke from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be. Aphra had come across intel about Adelhard seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Anoat sector like he had been. Luke and Rynn breaking for their starfighters, Zenat stopped Skywalker, asking what was wrong. He said he was longing for the simplicity of Rynn's life as a pilot, which caused Zenat to keep asking. Luke then expressed his regret at not confirming Adelhard's demise on the
Scepter
, as the Imperial tried to trick the eager Arcanist with an artifact, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.
Targeted by Adelhard
"
Back away from him, assassin. You only get one shot to strike at the heart of the Republic—and you missed.
"
―Commander Elara standing down Kranor, the bounty hunter sent to kill Kaelen Vos on Chandrila
right
As Skywalker had caused trouble for Moff Adelhard in the Anoat sector, the Imperial warlord placed a bounty on the young Arcanist. After the mission to Lehanis, former
Cloud-Rider
Weazel
brought information about the bounty on Skywalker to Adelhard's Spice Runner allies, but
Zeva Bliss
revealed that she was already aware of Adelhard targeting Skywalker. This was a surprise to Bliss's lieutenant Tomasso, who then decided to leak this information to New Republic spy
Calder Behrens
to protect Skywalker. Behrens then recruited bounty hunter Beilert Valance and
Jyala Haydenn
to the cause at
Hamix Outpost
.
Skywalker and Kranor duel on Chandrila.
Back on Chandrila, Skywalker had returned to his sister to inform her about what Doctor Aphra had told him: Adelhard wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Behrens, Mon Mothma burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened,
Trandoshan
bounty hunter and spice runner
Kranor
crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Skywalker ignited his Plasma Edge and dueled the hulking hunter's vibroblade. As the two fought, Kranor mentioned the recent death of senator
Mirim Kess
before powerfully slashing and knocking down Skywalker. As Kranor raised his blaster at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Leia, giving Luke enough time to rise and strike back with his Plasma Edge. As the Skywalker twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Luke asked who he worked for. Kranor then dove at and attacked Mothma before the pregnant Organa blasted him. Skywalker then struck once more, causing the Trandoshan to leap back out the window after telling Luke that the message was that no one is safe, not even him. This attack was followed up by another where
Lan-Drus
killed Calder Behrens, threatening Skywalker.
Striking back
"
There's a madman trying to undercut our goal of establishing this Republic. Do we think by
ignoring
him he'll go away? You heart what Lando said—They barely got out alive. If we just hope Adelhard disappears, we'll be sorely disappointed sooner than we'd like.
"
―Kaelen Vos discussing plans on Chandrila
In the Anoat sector, Lando Calrissian was still leading a task force fighting against Adelhard's Iron Blockade, but Chandrila had heard little from them, leading to unease from Admiral Ackbar and Mon Mothma. The galaxy was in chaos and war still raged, and the New Republic simply didn't have enough ships to finish off the Empire like they wanted to. Suddenly, two ships entered Chandrilan airspace: Calrissian and the pilot Preeti had returned from the Anoat sector with news. Meanwhile, Skywalker discussed military deployments with his sister. Organa was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Ackbar to offer more ships to the campaign against Adelhard, or just leaving him alone. Skywalker tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Adelhard was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke. Then, Zenat entered, getting Skywalker and Organa for a meeting called by Mothma regarding the information Calrissian brought in.
Skywalker explains his plan for dealing with the situation in the Anoat sector.
Mothma informed the group that Behrens had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. This new intelligence revealed that Reyna Oskure was not as firmly allied to Adelhard as they once though, but also that the Imperial remnant, led by the mysterious
Gallius Rax
, was seeking to confront the New Republic at
Jakku
in a
final confrontation
. The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Jakku and monitoring the situation in the Anoat sector. A debate began, with Skywalker adamantly advocating for taking action against Adelhard, but Ackbar and Mothma didn't know if it was possible. Practically begging, Luke asked if Ackbar would permit himself, Calrissian, Zenat, and more taking a small attack force to the Anoat sector, using this as a chance to undercut Adelhard's coalition with the Acolytes and the underworld. In Calder Behrens' memory, Skywalker said it was time they struck back against the mad Moff Adelhard.
Skywalker gathers his forces near the Iron Blockade.
Soon after, on the fringes of the Anoat sector, Skywalker gathered his forces with Valance, Haydenn, Preeti, and Zenat, preparing to get past the Iron Blockade and bring the fight to Adelhard. Valance had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Imperial sensor network from a smuggler he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Skywalker and Valance spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Adelhard had almost crushed an entire
Uprising
in his sector. The Moff had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Valance's friend, so this was personal for him too.
Skywalker's fleet engages the
Scepter
.
The New Republic's fleet found entering into the Anoat sector easier than they expected, thanks to Valance's friend's information, and launched an attack on the
Scepter
, Adelhard's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent mutiny against Adelhard led by Oskure with the help of the Spice Runners of Kijimi. Skywalker's forces rapidly engaged the
Scepter
, led by Luke's Star-Dart, striking hard while the Void Crusader's crew was caught unawares. Then, Preeti noticed something launch from the
Scepter
, but Zenat dismissed it as debris. A broadcast then went out to New Republic forces: the voice of Reyna Oskure said that Ubrik Adelhard was dead. The Anzati witch was insistent that Skywalker's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Anoat sector and had Imperial reinforcements coming to bolster her forces. Upon hearing this, Calrissian told Skywalker he'd received word from Ackbar saying to pull back, but Lando was wary of this choice. Skywalker and Zenat then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Skywalker's forces there pulled out. Little did they know, though, Moff Adelhard was not killed. The man had left the
Scepter
in an escape pod with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.
Galowyss grapple
"
And after what happened in this sector—how close
Adelhard
came to derailing what we were trying to build... part of me needs to be sure he's gone. Forever.
"
―Kaelen Vos talking to Rynn Zenat on Galowyss.
Skywalker and Zenat face off against Adelhard loyalist stormtroopers.
After the fleet left, Valance left Skywalker and Zenat with a ship to continue searching for Adelhard. After two days, over Galowyss, the Luke and Rynn discussed the blip that was picked up near the
Scepter
. Zenat was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Skywalker was insistent that Adelhard was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good. They then landed on the rocky world, where Skywalker confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him. Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Adelhard had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his stormtroopers had spotted the Arcanist and pilot. The mad Moff insisted that he was no longer a believer like Skywalker, and sent his forces to destroy the Arcanist, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.
Adelhard flees Galowyss.
Soon after, the stormtroopers engaged Skywalker and Zenat, and the two fought fiercely, Luke deflecting countless blaster bolts back at the former Imperials. Skywalker pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a trooper received a communication saying their ship was leaving. Hearing the trooper give this order, Skywalker and Zenat sprinted after them, coming upon a
Lambda
-class shuttle being loaded. Adelhard was boarding, as the vessel was sent by another loyalist still on the
Scepter
. Seeing this, Skywalker and Zenat rushed to the shuttle intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it. Adelhard was once again in the air, departing Galowyss with his loyalists. Frustrated, Skywalker and Zenat ran back to their ship. Once onboard, they received a transmission from Organa. She told them that there had been a
bombing on Chandrila
, and that Luke needed to return immediately. He told his sister that Adelhard was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Acolytes of the Beyond, meaning it was Adelhard's doing. The madman, now in command of the
Scepter
and coalition once more, was seeking revenge.
The two then took off, and Zenat questioned if they were even going to make it back to Chandrila in time to help with anything. But Skywalker reassured her that they could still do good there, as Leia was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Adelhard's now cornered and desperate forces.
Pieces falling into place
"
"We"?
We
have to head to Jakku. The battle has started—the Empire has unleashed
everything
on us. The Operator wanted us there—it was a trap. Now our friends are stranded.
"
―Kaelen Vos talking to Rynn Zenat on Chandrila, after the Battle of Jakku had begun.
The Skywalker twins are reunited with Jax Rigger and Mon Mothma.
In a tower on Chandrila, Mothma, Organa, agent
Bastion
, and the recently returned Jax Rigger were waiting for Skywalker as he and Zenat entered the room. There was a brief and joyous reunion between the former-Rebel heroes, before the discussion shifted to Adelhard. Organa then told Skywalker about the recent attack and Bastion told him about the
Dowutin
Acolyte
Saycos Hawn
that was captured. The agent was concerned with Adelhard's resurgence, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Acolytes and the Spice Runners– and she wished luck to Lando's forces still fighting in the Anoat sector. Organa then interrogated Hawn, and as she left the chamber with news of Adelhard's plan to tip the scales at Jakku, Skywalker and the rest received a transmission. It was from the undercover Kith Alaytia, and confirmed what Leia had just heard: Adelhard had his sights set on Jakku, and wanted to tear down both the New Republic and Rax's Imperial remnant forces alike.
Soon after, the Empire and New Republic's forces were gathering at Jakku. The New Republic Senate had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Solo and Mothma, and the end of the war was about to begin.
From Chandrila, Skywalker and the rest monitored the situation on Jakku, particularly the consolidation of the Imperial fleet around the Super Void Crusader
Ravager
. Gallius Rax gave a rallying speech, and the gigantic battle began, Adelhard's forces still in the Anoat sector preparing.
Meanwhile, Skywalker and Zenat stood on a landing pad to meet the newly-escaped Alaytia and Kranor, the assassin who had previously targeted Mothma. Rynn knew they were coming, and Skywalker was confused by this. Skywalker was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Jakku, but instead they were meeting on Chandrila. Luke wanted to fight Adelhard himself, but Zenat assured him that there was another way. Kranor then said that Adelhard relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Luke wasn't inclined to believe because Kranor was a Spice Runner. But then, Zenat revealed that she herself was a Spice Runner as well, surprising Skywalker. Zenat told him that she knew how to take down Adelhard's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.
Zenat leads Skywalker to Sauwecer to make moves against Adelhard.
On the planet
Sauwecer
, the group landed and Skywalker learned that the Spice Runner Tomasso was Zenat's adopted father after her parents were killed by the Empire. She was raised to become a Spice Runner, but left to join the Rebellion instead. Then, Tomasso himself emerged, confusing Kranor and Alaytia about where the Spice Runners stood in regard to Adelhard. Zenat then asked her father to pull back the Spice Runners, and Skywalker begged the man to help them. Tomasso explained that Zeva Bliss was not blindly allied with Adelhard, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Spice Runners against him. But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Adelhard– he needed their help too.
Pirate ally & infiltration
"
Well, 'bout time you showed up,
Skywalker
—the party's getting good.
"
―Eleodie Maracavanya while fighting the Adelhard coalition in the Anoat sector.
Eleodie Maracavanya's Super Void Crusader
Liberty's Misrule
engaged Adelhard's forces before they went to Jakku.
As the Battle of Jakku raged, Tomasso spoke to Zeva Bliss and guaranteed that the Spice Runners would turn on Adelhard. Then, in the meantime, Skywalker and Zenat arrived in the Anoat sector, where Adelhard's forces were staging for the hyperspace jump to Jakku. There, they came upon pirate ruler
Eleodie Maracavanya
and zher Super Void Crusader
Liberty's Misrule
, which had arrived to take on Adelhard. Maracavanya greeted Skywalker, and his ship was fired upon by Adelhard's vessels until a rogue TIE fired on his attackers. This was Therisa Alern, still working to free the Anoat sector. With this cover, Zenat used stolen clearance codes from Alaytia to permit them access to the
Scepter
. The shuttle haphazardly landed in its hangar as Alern was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zenat. Skywalker then reminded her that they needed to calmly take this chance, this incredible opportunity to make Alern's sacrifice mean something more. Skywalker and Zenat were now in place on the
Scepter
, ready to take down Adelhard, and Maracavanya contacted Luke to let him know that zhe was taking too much damage. After essentially delaying the mad Moff, the pirate regent departed, as Adelhard's fleet prepared to jump to Jakku.
Arcanist at Jakku
"
Your ship is falling apart, Adelhard. I didn't come to fight you. I came to
save
you.
"
―Skywalker speaking to Urbik Adelhard on the bridge of the
Scepter
during the battle of Jakku
Jakku's orbit was completely filled with vessels, the Imperial fleet making a ring around the
Ravager
and facing off against the New Republic's
Starhawk
battleships. As Starhawk
Amity
was rammed and destroyed by Void Crusader
Punishment
,
Adelhard's fleet arrived, Skywalker onboard its lead ship. Just when he posed the greatest threat, Adelhard was backstabbed by Reyna Oskure and the Spice Runners at the same time. The Void Crusader was falling apart, Zenat met up with the fleeing Anzati in the
Scepter'
s halls, and Skywalker made his way toward the bridge. As the once-mighty
Ravager
fell to Jakku and the New Republic began to emerge victorious, Skywalker confronted the mad self-declared Grand Moff Adelhard for the last time.
Adelhard and Skywalker duel as the old state of the galaxy burns down around them.
Entering the burning bridge with his saber ignited, Skywalker found a kneeling, battered man, consumed by his own madness, crouching in front of him. Adelhard questioned Luke's presence there, saying that he'd already won– but the young Arcanist told the old Imperial that he wasn't there to fight him, he was there to save him as his ship was falling apart. Skywalker told him there was another path, one averse to darkness, but Adelhard responded to this with only a mad laugh at the idea of being saved. Insisting he was beyond salvation, the man swung at Skywalker with his
Vibro-ax
In the
Scepter'
s collapsing docking bay, Skywalker and Zenat ran out to meet the
Star Strider
– Jax Rigger had left Chandrila to join the battle and rescue them. The two boarded the old freighter, and remarked that it finally, truly felt like this was over. The
Scepter
exploded, and with it, Adelhard's dreams of conquest, as the heroes flew off, victorious, into a new era.
War's end
"
He's
beautiful
, Leia--I'm so happy for you both...the three of you.
"
"
How does it feel to be an uncle, Luke? Maybe that'll keep you close and stop your wandering.
"
―Kaelen Vos and Commander Elara discussing the birth of Ben
Skywalker attended the signing of the Galactic Concordance between the The Hegemony and the New Republic on Chandrila
While the Battle of Jakku lingered on the desert world's surface, the Galactic Civil War came to an end on Chandrila with the signing of the
Galactic Concordance
, a peace treaty between the Republic and the Empire that forced the Empire to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military force had disappeared into the
Unknown Regions
.
. Skywalker, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Concordance between Mas Amedda and Mon Mothma overlooking Hanna City.
Skywalker visited his family shortly after the birth of his nephew Ben.
Following the signing of the treaty, Leia gave birth to her and Han's son and Luke's nephew,
Ben Solo
, on Chandrila. Skywalker visited the family shortly after Ben's birth where Leia hoped that Skywalker being an uncle would keep him closer to home.
An
Imperial officer
Reconstruction
Freedom for the Nagai
Fenril pirate attacks
"
