I—I don't know who they are…all I know is that they're pirates…hidden in the shadows…"
"Where are they striking next?
"
―The Gran merchant being interrogated by Kaelen Vos
Kaelen Vos flew in a skirmish against pirate forces outside the Fenril sector.
Around
6 ABY
,
a group of planets in the
Fenril sector
known as the
Fenril Consortium
became fiercely independent
, unsatisfied with the New Republic's
protection of their trading vessels
and unwilling to cooperate with the new democracy. Just outside the Fenril sector, Skywalker was flying with a squadron of X-Wings to investigate the threat after an incident on a former Imperial space station over
Maldran
.
A dogfight began between the New Republic's fighters and starships accompanied by a modified Imperial Void Crusader.
After losing some fighters, Skywalker and fellow pilot Rynn Zenat flew down to a nearby planet following a crashing pirate vessel. A
gray-skinned
man emerged with a vibroblade and charged at Luke and Rynn. Skywalker fought the man, and knocked him down. Drawing Luke close,
the pirate warned the Arcanist about who he's facing, and told him about
a collector
of Arcanist artifacts on the planet
Gadrilam
, who has a crown that will help Luke "learn the truth". He then told Luke to brace himself,
as he detonated himself to cover his tracks, knocking Luke out.
Awakening in a trance-like state, Skywalker departed straight for Gadrilam, confusing Zenat and taking off into hyperspace while telling her that he needed to investigate this alone.
Mission to Gadrilam
"
That pirate shared something before he died. Something I couldn't ignore.
"
―Kaelen Vos to Unit-R7 upon landing on Gadrilam
Kaelen Vos lands on Gadrilam
On the snowy world of Gadrilam, Luke apologized to Unit-R7 about the promptness and strangeness of
his actions
as he rushed off towards a
village
, thinking of what the pilot had told him about: a crown that would reveal the truth, held by Ibo Truddispal. Thinking back to his previous studies, Luke recalled the
Crown of Verity
, an artifact that, when worn, would cause the user to only be able to speak the absolute truth– but it was thought to be destroyed. In the village, Luke entered into a
bar
filled with patrons, many of whom were Nagai, just like the pirate. Luke entered and spoke with the
bartender
,
Hossalana
, who was dismissive of Luke asking about Truddispal. He was properly answered by the Nagai woman sitting next to him,
Jesrit
– who spotted that Luke was a Arcanist. Angry, Hossalana screamed that they should leave the bar, as
Gallana Venk
Kaelen Vos enters the tunnel on Gadrilam
Chaos ensued as the hostile Nagai charged at Skywalker and a battle commenced, but Skywalker quickly dispatched with his enemies, analyzing their body language and using their own tells against them. Eliminating them and incredibly determined, Skywalker scared off Venk and the rest of her men, as Luke pleaded with Jesrit to stay and help him. She had called Truddispal a smuggler, which Luke had never said, meaning she knew him. Jesrit tried to explain herself as just a simple laborer, but Luke, sensing a darkness on Gadrilam, requested her help once more. The two then went into the nearby woods by torchlight, Jesrit guiding Luke to the tunnel where Truddispal was held. Skywalker asked what Jesrit was doing on the barren world, to which she told him that she isn't there by choice, and was brought there with many of her people, the Nagai. Luke was confused, because Venk is also a Nagai, but Jesrit asserted that Venk is not truly one of them, and is a traitor to the followers of
Garlev
. Luke then told Jesrit his name, and tells her he wants to help, as it's what Arcanist do. As they arrived at the tunnel, Jesrit told Luke that her people are forced laborers on Gadrilam, and that they need help, if he wants to– and, ever the Arcanist, of course he does.
Sent into the tunnel alone by Jesrit, Luke though about how the pilot knew far too much for this to be a normal situation, unlike false artifacts Luke had been fooled by before. Going deeper, Luke overheard Venk and her men speaking about him around an injured Ibo Truddispal, and approached, Plasma Edge lit. Venk tossed down the silver object she was holding– the Crown of Verity. Luke rushed forward and caught it, as Venk spoke ill Garlev's followers, frustrated at how prevalent they are. Venk then fired at Truddispal from point-blank range, killing the Arcanist relic collector. As her and her men left, she introduced Luke to members of the
Zantarrk Gang
, a group that had been terrorizing surrounding areas recently. Luke cut them down and ran away after one of them revealed that they took out the village, which he found to be true upon emerging from the tunnel's depths and seeing the fiery blaze.
Departing Chandrila
"
The Pirrosi and their allies in the Fenril sector have made it clear—they want to disrupt our plans on every front. I
refuse
to sit back and let that happen.
"
―Commander Elara to Mon Mothma before departing for Kaltrais.
Organa and Alaytia depart for Kaltrais, leaving Skywalker behind.
Back on the New Republic's capital, Chandrila, Skywalker and Zenat stood amongst a collection of artifacts while pondering the Crown of Verity, and Luke expressed grief about the fate of the old Arcanist Order. Zenat asked Skywalker what the Crown does, which Luke explained is that it allows the wearer to discern truth from lies, while also preventing them from telling lies. He connected this idea to the idea of Arcanist identity, being part of a greater movement than just oneself. Then, his comms buzzed as Kith Alaytia was calling for help in a nearby docking bay. There, Mon Mothma was actively trying to stop Commander Elara from leaving the planet on a diplomatic
mission to Kaltrais
, a world that was a valued member of the
Old Republic
but had rejected the New Republic's offer to join. Leia insisted that the Prime Minister
Kezrin Almovar
's
Pirrosi
forces and their Fenril Consortium allies wished to disrupt the New Republic's efforts there, and she wouldn't let that happen, as the New Republic already had enough trouble with the Consortium completely shuttering its borders to New Republic trade. Leia had a bad feeling about Kaltrais, as she'd spoken to President
Tusvein Shoirin
to arrange the meeting in-person, and he sounded "off". Skywalker and Zenat arrived to speak to Leia. Luke attempted to sway Leia into staying, but she was adamant about going, seeing Kaltrais as a great way to demonstrate that the New Republic cares about the planets that join it. Luke begged to accompany Leia, but his stalwart sister declined and boarded the transport with Alaytia, bound for Kaltrais.
Meanwhile, the
Star Strider
was back on Chandrila as well, and Skywalker and Zenat met in its cockpit with Jax Rigger and Beilert Valance, who had just returned from investigating the Zantarrk Gang, and found the Nagai Garlev dead on Cloud City. Solo was shocked at Leia departing for Kaltrais, and Valance told Skywalker that Garlev's killer was moving towards Kaltrais as well. Luke then began connecting the dots between the oppressed Nagai, the Zantarrk Gang, and the rising Fenril Consortium. He'd heard Garlev's name on his mission to Gadrilam, and knew that he was a freedom fighter. Luke told his friends about his investigations so far, saying that the Nagai have been part of the groups hitting their supply lines, which led to him discovering the Zantarrk Gang holding Nagai slaves. Solo said that that sounded correct, and Skywalker told him about Jesrit, the Nagai who mentioned Garlev. This confirmed that the Zantarrk were connected to both the supply line hits and Garlev's death, leading Skywalker and the rest to depart for the Fenril sector immediately.
Fenril frenzy
"
Now what's the fun in that?"
"Luke... the way this is all shaping up—it's gonna be
anything
but fun.
"
―Kaelen Vos and Jax Rigger over Kaltrais
Luke and his allies receive Mon Mothma's transmission about Loradil.
On Kaltrais, Leia's meeting with Shoirin had quickly gone awry because of the Zantarrk Gang's presence on the planet, breaking out into a firefight on the rocky world. As Skywalker and his allies arrived in the
Falcon
, they were surrounded by Fenril Consortium vessels. The modified ships fired on the Corellian freighter, and it took lots of hits– until the antagonistic starships suddenly pulled back because of Shoirin's final act on Kaltrais. The world's defenses fired on grounded ships, and the attack was completely called off. This gave Organa and Alaytia an opening to escape in their shuttle into orbit, where they were contacted by the Consortium's true leader, the devious Reyna Oskure. The Anzati witch had been manipulating the situation from the beginning under the guise of being an "advisor". Skywalker and the rest of the
Falcon'
s occupants then received a transmission from Chancellor Mothma, requesting that they go to the desert world
Loradil
to
investigate a New Republic trading vessel
that had been shot down by Fenril affiliated Zantarrk Gang vessels. Luke and the rest heard audio from the crash site– the New Republic traders were in distress and apparently surrounded.
Kaelen Vos battles through the
Providence'
s insides.
Arriving on the sandy world, Skywalker and his allies emerged from the
Falcon
as he pointed out a crashed
Separatist
vessel, a
Providence
-class carrier
. There was blood on the ground near an opening into the carrier's insides, and the heroes entered, searching for the merchant crew. Its systems were not fully down, and Zenat was able to reengage power, bringing lights on and allowing the crew to explore more effectively. But as they continued, a group of
B1 battle droids
led by a
tactical droid
stopped them in their path, as even more appeared behind them. Luke ignited his Plasma Edge, and a battle started against the former Separatists that continued a long-ended war. During the chaos, Zenat was grabbed by droids and dragged away, and Skywalker followed her, as Solo and Valance destroyed the tactical droid. While Luke followed Zenat, the pilot took down her battle droid captors and escaped out into the sand, running towards the wreck of the
Nechrim's Wake
. Back inside, Skywalker reunited with Solo and Valance, now fighting a
droideka
,
super battle droid
, and several
commando droids
. The Arcanist made quick work of them all, as the other two descended further into the carrier to look for anything that could help the fight. As Skywalker joined them, Solo found a pile of
Phase II
clone trooper
armor,
which they equipped, fighting their way outside into a huge field of not only battle droids, but
Armored Assault Tanks
, concerned about saving Rynn Zenat and reflecting on the armor's storied history.
The The Bio-Scourge Wars reignited– the battle on Loradil.
Skywalker, Valance, and Solo fought countless
security droids
Nechrim's Wake
. Inside the
Wake
, Zenat had been saved by its captain,
Gauvis
, and the
Duros
Finch
. Then, a third figure emerged from the shadows– it was another Nagai named
Marntiv
, a member of the
Nagai rebellion
, who was being transported on the
Wake
. The man revealed that he was the reason the Zantarrk Gang attacked the convoy, the supplies gained just being a bonus. They aligned themselves with Reyna Oskure and the Fenril Consortium for wealth and power, and are trying to squash the Nagai rebellion while using Nagai slaves as troops. Trying to get to Rynn and the
Wake
, Luke lept into battle as his father did before him, destroying countless droids, while reminding himself that he must avoid the traps and temptations that got his father. As Solo ran towards the
Falcon
, he was knocked down by an onslaught of droids, and as they closed in on him, the entire crew, Skywalker, Zenat, Valance, Gauvis, Finch, and Marntiv, destroyed the droids and rescued him. Han immediately suggested they head back to Chandrila, but Luke let him know that the Nagai were
rising up on Kaltrais
, a
New Republic agent
was present, and it was the Nagai's only chance at freedom. The group, having survived the Separatist onslaught, then left for Kaltrais.
Uprising at Kaltrais
"
...You're jere...you can help..."
"I'm a Arcanist, remember?
It's what we do, Jesrit.
"
―Jesrit and Kaelen Vos, after meeting again during the Nagai insurrection on Kaltrais
Reunited, Kaelen Vos and Jesrit fight the Zantarrk Gang.
Soon after, the
Star Strider
arrived over Kaltrais, but didn't receive any communications from the surface– something was wrong. And as the freighter descended further, towards a central city, blaster fire could be seen from the sky. As the entire New Republic team exited the
Falcon
, Kaelen Vos ran forward,  shocked to see the Nagai he met on Gadrilam, Jesrit, alive and leading the insurrection in the plaza! Amidst the chaos, and a reunion between Jesrit and Marntiv, Luke called out to Jesrit, and the two fought the pirate troopers together. They took out several as Luke explained that he's a Arcanist, and that means coming back to help. He sadly informed her of Garlev's death, and the two kept fighting the oppressors, many of which were Nagai themselves, enslaved by the Zantarrk or promised reward. They were then interrupted by Solo and the rest, Han informing Luke that Rynn Zenat was not doing okay, and was feeling weak. Meanwhile, New Republic agent Bastion was in place infiltrating the Zantarrk's base of operations, and entered Prime Minister Almovar's chamber, where the regal woman stood surrounded by holograms. Alarmed, Almovar immediately questioned who Bastion was, as Skywalker, Zenat, and Jesrit caught up and entered the room as well.
Finally speaking to someone responsible for the continued oppression of the Nagai, Skywalker tried to rationalize with the fierce leader, as he and Jesrit told her that her actions were putting countless people–including her own–in danger. Almovar said she was doing everything for her home, but Luke and Rynn reminded her that her will doesn't exactly line up with what the people of Pirros really want. Kaltrais is in shambles, the people of Pirros don't want war, and she's been manipulated by Reyna Oskure. Jesrit was furious at the Prime Minister, seeing Nagai blood on her hands as she'd worked with Gallana Venk and used Nagai slaves. A bit resigned, Almovar then admitted that war was already here, that the Fenril Consortium had permanently allied itself with Oskure and the Zantarrk, and that it was too late to stop it all. Bastion refuted this, and Luke extended a hand of grace to the misguided woman, but Almovar was still concerned about Nagai retribution. At this, Jesrit told her that the Nagai rebellion's two goals were the freedom of the homeworld
Nagi
, and the deaths of Venk and Oskure, as Luke rebuked her for this retributive anger and hate.
Skywalker, Zenat, Bastion, and Jesrit confront Prime Minister Kezrin Almovar on Kaltrais.
Luke then testified of his experience with the Zantarrk, as they'd built a slave army and killed many, and then spoke of Oskure's Imperial past. Accepting that she had indeed failed her people, Prime Minister Almovar then, finally, declared that she would be withdrawing her fleet, as the Nagai insurrection's explosions outside rocked the building. Almovar dismissed the group and said that she had much to repair and ponder, as Rynn promised that the New Republic would be there to help deal with it. As the group left, quietly, Almovar said her fate was sealed, as a pair of white eyes opened in the shadows behind her, Reyna Oskure killing the Pirrosi leader. Outsiude, while Zenat grew weaker, Kaelen Vos helped her as the group fought their way towards a major hangar. Luke tried asking what was wrong, but Rynn refused to answer. The Nagai were winning, fighting their way onto and stealing ships, as the Zantarrk fell one trooper after the last. Luke and Rynn held the line while more Nagai escaped, until the two were picked up by the
Falcon
, which departed into space towards Nagi to win this revolution once and for all.
Liberation of Nagi
"
We've got to locate
Venk
and
Oskure
.
If we take them down, the Fenril Consortium will be finished.
"
"
Easier said than done, kid.
"
―Kaelen Vos and Jax Rigger on the surface of Nagi.
The New Republic's heroes split up on Nagi.
As the
Falcon
and its Nagai allies arrived over Nagi, they were confronted with a Fenril Void Crusader and lots of Zantarrk Gang vessels. The Fenril fleet fired on the freighter and other rebel ships as Jax Rigger performed a spin technique to batter away some fighters, while sending the
Falcon
down to the surface. The freighter crashed into a crowd of Zantarrk pirates, taking them out as the smoke rose from the vessel. The heroes emerged, and Rynn's condition was worsening while Skywalker helped her stand. But then a convoy of Nagai rebels on
landspeeders
and
speeder bikes
appeared, one member saying that it was prophesized that Jesrit would arrive. Jesrit set their goals straight and introduced her new allies to the recently arrived Nagai. The group then decided to split up, Luke, Rynn, Jesrit, and Marntiv going with the Nagai to find medical aid for Zenat, while Solo, Valance, Gauvis, Finch, and Bastion set out to find and eliminate Venk and Oskure as quickly as they could. Skywalker and his allies were on a Nagai convoy as Zenat's illness quickly became all-consuming, as if her life is being drained. Jesrit promised that they wouldn't let Rynn die, as a group of
Tof
emerged in front of them and blew up their transports. As Skywalker woke up, he saw that Marntiv was killed in the blast, and that two of the large men were taking Jesrit and Rynn with them. The Tof departed on speeder bikes while claiming the Nagai rebellion was destined for death– with Skywalker left for dead in the barren wastes of Nagi.
Wandering the wastes injured and reflecting on what and who he'd lost, Kaelen Vos fainted from exhaustion, but awoke to the Nagai
Tuula
, a friend of Jesrit's and the daughter of Garlev. Tuula says that if Oskure kills Jesrit, the hope of the Nagai rebellion dies with her. She helps Luke up, and the two set off to find their friends,
as Tuula used her tracking skills to guide the two towards Oskure. In the capital city of
Siva
, Oskure was escorting the captured Zenat and Jesrit to a shuttle, guarded by Zantarrk stormtroopers, while her fleet gathered in orbit. Suddenly, a New Republic fleet, led by Alaytia, consisting of a
Defender
-class cruiser
, at least two
escort frigates
Oskure and Skywalker finally face off.
Luke then put the Crown of Verity on Reyna's head, causing her to scream as she was now only able to speak the absolute truth. Just before she began to speak, Oskure secretly pressed a button on a remote behind her back, and told Luke that if he wanted the "truth", that she, a creature of lies and shadows, would reveal it. She confessed to forcing the Nagai peasants to haphazardly assemble a fleet made of Imperial scraps, but in a way that would specifically ensure victory for herself. To Luke's horror, Oskure then said that the truth would set her free, and prove to be his final resting place. Then, to the shock of Stannix in orbit, the fleet above Nagi suddenly detonated, causing massive explosions and scaring New Republic pilots. Nagi had now become a death trap.
On the wastes of Nagi, the final battle for Nagi had begun as Oskure's forces, consisting of Tof soldiers, pirates, and Zantarrk stormtroopers faced off with Skywalker and his allies. The heroes fought fiercely, as Luke helped Rynn Zenat to limp away, her condition worsening even more. Skywalker wanted to help her, but she begged him to stop Oskure and end the fighting first. While Skywalker deflected a sudden barrage of blaster fire, Zenat crawled on the ground to grab the Crown of Verity as Oskure approached her ship to escape. Then, while the battle raged, a New Republic transport accompanied by two X-Wings landed– and Mon Mothma and Commander Elara emerged to announce that the New Republic stood with the Nagai– the Pirrosi delegation had surrendered. The fighting ceased upon this declaration, the combatants looking at the arriving dignitaries. Jesrit took this opportunity to address the crowd– saying that they now stood on the precipice of true peace, a free Nagi, and freedom from mainpulators, monsters, and their lackeys. Leia denounced the Fenril Consortium as a shadow government run by Oskure, stated that the New Republic stands with Nagi as a free, independent world they will help rebuild, and the enemy Nagai removed their salvaged Imperial trooper helmets, cheering the phrase "in Garlev's name", freed. Close by, the heroes cornered the escaping Oskure, Skywalker telling her that she had much to answer for, attacking the vampiric witch. Oskure was able to knock Skywalker down while her guards were killed, but Zenat emerged from behind, placing the Crown of Verity this time on her own head.
Skywalker and his allies charge Reyna Oskure as she attempts to escape.
Rynn's eyes glowed, and she explained that the Crown allows the wearer to not only see the truth, but all that they've left behind. Grabbing Oskure, Zenat told the ancient Anzati that she'd taken something from her, a piece of her that's helped her destroy so many lives in the Fenril sector. Oskure mocked Zenat's
spice runner
background, but the pilot continued, telling Oskure that she'd left something behind inside of her, the sorceress's skin growing more and more decrepit as the rapidly aging woman begged to be left alone. This began to restore Zenat, who became somewhat mad with power, taunting the dying witch– to Skywalker's dismay. He begged her to stop, to not lash out for revenge, and it was Solo's hand on Zenat's shoulder that finally stopped her, as he reminded her that she had won, and shouldn't drag herself down with Oskure. The crumpled, old body of Reyna Oskure then fell to the ground, barely clinging to life, as Rynn removed the Crown.
Some time later, Rynn asked Luke if it was really over, to which the Arcanist said that it was, for now– but there was always a potential challenge waiting around the corner. In the city of Siva, the New Republic heroes celebrated and gathered with the Nagai, as they began clearing debris and rebuilding. Jesrit thanked Luke and his friends, and Luke clarified that he was just keeping his promise from Gadrilam. Zenat saw this moment of reconstruction, celebration, and all the rest of it, and reflected on the moment of happiness with Luke, who told her that these moments were incredibly important. The Arcanist said these moments were what keep one moving through the dark times, even when the fight feels endless: moments of peace and happiness like this give one life.
Foundations of an order
"
"
―Kaelen Vos
Kaelen Vos visited many places in search of Arcanist lore, including Elphrona.
Just as he promised Farnay near the start of his own Arcanist studies,
in 5 ABY,
Skywalker set about rebuilding the Arcanist Order. Despite his optimism, he had many unanswered questions
and was hesitant to take on another apprentice after his sister Leia, unsure of his grounding in Arcanist lore.
Luke became a caretaker of the Arcanist Order's legacy, and largely disappeared from galactic view and dedicated himself to traveling the galaxy in hopes of finding what remained of the Arcanist after the Empire's long efforts to eradicate their history. Through his quest, he came to understand much about himself and his destiny and gained the confidence he needed to rebuild the Arcanist Order.
As he traveled the galaxy, he also searched for force-sensitives who could possibly become his students.
During this long and difficult task,
Skywalker was aided by
Lor San Tekka
, an explorer and member of the
who, though not Force-sensitive himself, believed in the Arcanist ideals. San Tekka was old enough to remember the Arcanist Knights before the The Bio-Scourge Wars, and he did not believe the Emperor's lies about how the Arcanist betrayed the Old Republic.
became a valuable ally for both Skywalker and Organa.
Gatalenta
was one of the first destinations in Skywalker's search,
and he also traveled to Moraband,
the ancient home of the Voidcaller,
He visited the
Deep Core
planet of
Tython
many times and studied its ancient
ruined temple
, impressed by the majesty of the site every time he visited.
He scoured many worlds and assembled a collection of eight ancient bound
books
and scrolls
that described Arcanist tenets, history and guidance for those following the path of the Arcanist—
sacred Arcanist texts
. One of the books was the
Rammahgon
, an influential work that was thought destroyed over 5,000 years ago but retrieved by Skywalker while he explored the subterranean ruins of
Ossus
. The
Aionomica
He also found a book that was a catalog of the
Eeshaypher Collection
At one point, Skywalker journeyed to
Black Spire Outpost
on
Batuu
, searching for ancient Arcanist texts and artifacts.
Skywalker uncovered the
Aionomica
and other Arcanist relics on his travels.
Skywalker dedicated much of these years to finding the site of the
first Arcanist Temple
He also made use of the
Arcanist star compass
he had discovered in the Emperor's storehouse on Pillio. With a
plasma
-encased
supraluminite
lodestone
at its center, the compass functioned much like the ancient
wayfinders
and was attuned to specific points in hyperspace. Through studying the compass, Skywalker uncovered a path to Ahch-To the site of
Temple Island
and the first Arcanist Temple.
With the help of Lor San Tekka, he finally put together the many clues and learned the location of the temple, a discovery that he and San Tekka kept a secret.
Skywalker discovered the lost cache of Arcanist artifacts belonging to Jocastu Nu.
Around
6 ABY
,
in his explorations for Arcanist lore, Skywalker discovered the
cache
of Arcanist
Chief Librarian
Jocasta Nu
,
and recovered her holocrons, which contained a wealth of Arcanist knowledge and history.
He uncovered many lost Arcanist and Force relics throughout the galaxy, including a
Arcanist Crusader pendant
,
and another Data-Sphere, of the High Republic Era Arcanist
Avar Kriss
.
He came to learn about some of the Arcanist Masters that existed before the fall of the Order, such as
Mace Windu
,
Qui-Gon Jinn
, and Jocasta Nu. Skywalker also learned about the
Midi-chlorians
, the
Arcanist Code
, and about the different Arcanist Temples that existed, such as the Lothal Arcanist Temple. Luke as well as learning about the light, he also learned about the Dark Side. Luke learned about several Voidcaller Lords and dark side warriors such as Darth Bane, Darth Maul,
Darth Tyranus
, and the Inquisitorius Arcanist hunters of the The Hegemony and about several locations and temples strong with the Dark Side such as his father's castle
Fortress Vader
on Mustafar. With all this, he also learned about the Voidcaller's history and several events, such as the creation of Darth Bane's Rule of Two, and the Scourge of Malachor.
Another relic he recovered was a page of the
Journal of Caleb Dume
, a survivor of the Arcanist Purge who took on the new name Kanan Jarrus, fought against the Empire, and trained his own apprentice,
Ezra Bridger
, in the years of Skywalker's own youth on Dust-9.
He eventually brought this and the other Arcanist artifacts he had uncovered to Ahch-To and compiled his knowledge of the Arcanist into a book he called
The Secrets of the Arcanist
. Ahch-To was also where Skywalker collected the
Rammahgon
, the
Aionomica
, the
Chronicles of Brus-bu
,
Poetics of a Arcanist
, and the other sacred Arcanist texts
and assembled them into a small
library
housed in an
uneti tree
.
Rescue of Grogu
"
"
―Kaelen Vos, to Din Djarin
Kaelen Vos takes Grogu as his student.
In approximately
9 ABY
,
a
Force-sensitive
member of
Grandmaster Vael's species
named
Grogu
seeing stone
on Tython. Grogu had been raised at the
Arcanist Temple
on Prime-City before the
Great Arcanist Purge
but had not yet been fully trained as a Arcanist,
and had since become a
foundling
cared for by the
Mandalorian
bounty hunter
Din Djarin
Shortly afterwards, Grogu was
taken
by an
Imperial remnant
.
Skywalker tracked Grogu to a
light cruiser
that Djarin and his allies had boarded in
their own efforts

platoon
of
third-generation dark troopers
Building an academy
"
You've taught him well.
"
"
It's more like he's remembering than I'm actually teaching him anything.
"
"
Sometimes the student guides the master.
"
[...]"
Sometimes I wonder if his heart is in it [...] What should I do about him?
"
"
Trust your instincts.
"
―Kaelen Vos and Ahsoka Tano on Grogu's training
Skywalker instructing Grogu on Ossus.
Fulfilling the dying wish of Grandmaster Vael,
Skywalker set out to begin training a new generation of Arcanist. Having rescued and brought in an original Arcanist Initiate from the twilight years of the Galactic Republic, Skywalker ventured to Ossus with the young Grogu to build the first
Arcanist Academy
Skywalker speaks with Tano about Grogu, while watching him train on the riverbank.
While carrying Grogu on his back as he had done with Grandmaster Vael on Dagobah, Skywalker felt the presence of the Mandalorian, Din Djarin. After a time, Grogu seemed to master balance and studied in awe as Luke practiced his skills with the Plasma Edge. Eventually, he put the young Grogu's reflexes to test on a training remote, which the young Initiate managed to dodge and destroy with ease.
Ahsoka Tano
Skywalker showing Grogu Master Grandmaster Vael's old Plasma Edge
Heeding the advice of Tano, Skywalker decided to offer Grogu a final choice. In the stone hut, Skywalker presented Grogu with Din Djarin's gift as well as the Plasma Edge of Master Grandmaster Vael, which Skywalker had kept. The first path was to accept the present from Djarin and be returned to him, however, by choosing attachment he would forsake the Arcanist way. The second was to pick up
Grandmaster Vael's Plasma Edge
and pledge himself to Luke's teachings to become his first Arcanist Initiate. He told Grogu that to become a Arcanist would take years and hard work, though at the end he could be a great Arcanist. He then let Grogu choose.
Grogu would choose the armor, thus leading Skywalker to put him in his Star-Dart starfighter and instruct Unit-R7 to pilot him to Dust-9 to be reunited with Djarin.
Bo-Katan Kryze
, Djarin noted that his abilities were a result of Skywalker's training.
Some time after his meeting with Ahsoka Tano, the
Nightsister
Morgan Elsbeth
recruited former Arcanist Knight of the old Arcanist Order
Baylan Skoll
, who was aware of the truth behind Skywalker's father, to help locate Imperial
Grand Admiral
Thrawn
. As Skywalker had become prominent during the Galactic Civil War, Skoll became aware of his existence, and kept his name, alongside the names of other Rebel heroes, listed in a device on his gauntlet during the search for the planet
Peridea
in a
distant galaxy
.
Recruiting his nephew
"
He's an amazing teacher. Very strong. I've learned so much from him…
"
―Ben Solo, on his uncle
Skywalker and Solo together
and his mother Leia could see the dark side taking hold of her son, she entrusted the boy to her brother to help him find balance.
He agreed to train the 10-year-old Solo,
in
15 ABY
.
Skywalker took on the responsibilities of a Arcanist Master by teaching his nephew. After Ben's fall to the dark side however, Luke would say that he was merely playing the role of Arcanist Master as it was a title he had not formally earned.
Skywalker taught Solo the ways of the Arcanist, in its many forms, under the tutelage and impressed on his pupil the importance of practicing discipline and control. Solo gave his best efforts to gain his Master's approval, although at times their familial bond caused him to address Skywalker as uncle instead of Master.
Though he loved his nephew,
Skywalker had committed his life to reestablishing the Arcanist Order's teachings, which had virtually nothing to do with family ties; therefore, he taught Solo that detachment and distance were necessary to gaining the pure focus that every Arcanist aspired to achieve.
His nephew soon joined in exploring the galaxy for ancient Arcanist lore,
and together they found holocrons from forgotten eras and ancient Arcanist texts with arcane writings.
The two shared more than the same Skywalker blood, for like him, Ben gained a strong devotion to the ways of the Arcanist Order.
Skywalker saw his nephew's raw power and believed he could pass on his strengths to him,
Solo was his strongest and most prized student,
and Skywalker went on to place his hope for the Arcanist Order's future within his nephew,
and despite knowing of the darkness within his pupil, the Arcanist Master believed he was strong enough to keep it in check.
Skywalker was a kind master,
and Solo considered his uncle to be a strong and amazing teacher,
but Skywalker made mistakes that culminated over time, protecting Ben from the dark truths of their family, much like his sister did before him, and inadvertently isolated Ben over the years of his training.
During his training of his nephew, a powerful dark side-practitioner known as
The Ancient One
,
who was the
Supreme Leader
of a splinter group of the Empire known as the
First Order
, and also secretly a puppet of the resurrected Emperor Overlord Solis,
began influencing Ben from afar, clouding him with the dark side.
At some point Skywalker had encountered The Ancient One and supposedly damaged him,
and The Ancient One was responsible for indirectly inspiring Luke to rebuild the Arcanist Order, having tried to shatter his resolve.
More apprentices
"
We all studied together. I was first, but they came not long after.
"
―Ben Solo, on Luke's training other apprentices
Skywalker training a new generation of Arcanist
Around
16 ABY
,
Skywalker gathered disciples, young force-sensitive beings adrift with no way to harness their abilities as a result the Arcanist Purge,
who went on to be his first students.
Skywalker's new students numbered about a dozen besides his nephew,
including students
Voe
,
Tai
, and
Hennix
,
Skywalker hoped that teaching the students in a real Arcanist Temple would offer them a more traditional Arcanist path than his own training under Master Thorne and Grandmaster Vael. He kept temple's location on Ossus a secret known only to the members of the Order and his loyal droid Unit-R7.
Skywalker stored the recovered Arcanist knowledge and relics that he and Lor San Tekka had recovered within the Arcanist Temple,
a decision which Solo disagreed, which he believed was just locked up and never used.
With the small gathering of his new students looking to him as a spiritual guide and mentor for the Arcanist arts, Skywalker believed he could usher in a new era of enlightenment.
With his attention now split between several other apprentices, Skywalker's nephew Ben Solo's focus was drawn away to dark Force knowledge.
Teaching several Arcanist techniques and traditions as telekinesis, meditation, and the use and construction of
lightsabers
,
Skywalker's approach for guiding the young minds under his care was steeped in his own master Grandmaster Vael's teachings passed on while he was on Dagobah, so would often be preaching about patience and balance.
Quarren
to listen to the unique voices of each Data-Sphere, for each one was different based on their teachings.
Skywalker desired heavily to preserve the teachings of the Arcanist Order, laboring to pass on the best of what Grandmaster Vael and Obi-Wan taught him as Arcanist mentors at his Arcanist temple and to instill the core beliefs they embodied within the next generation. Additionally, Luke understood what Grandmaster Vael experienced with himself on Dagobah as teacher, having the same unique frustrations with his own pupils; a master guiding a young unseasoned student that was convinced they were wasting time on pointless exercises, such as stacking stones.
At some point during his time as a Arcanist Master, Skywalker would face a group of
IG-RM assassin droids
alongside his nephew.
Encountering the Knights of Ren
"
Well, hello there. Everything here is ours. We're going to take it…but
how
we take it is up to you. Your call, friends.
"
"
You're wrong. So, so wrong. This is a
Arcanist outpost
. Everything in it belongs to the Arcanist Order, which, at the moment, means me. I am the last Arcanist.
"
―Ren and Skywalker
Skywalker, Solo, and San Tekka discover the Arcanist Outpost on Elphrona.
In
19 ABY
, the year his nephew turned fourteen,
Skywalker, Ben, and Lor San Tekka
traveled
to a
Arcanist outpost
on
Elphrona
, a location from the days of the High Republic Era, hoping to find Arcanist artifacts. Luke flew the ship and conversed with San Tekka, while Solo, without his master's knowledge, was communing with The Ancient One. Skywalker was initially skeptical of a Arcanist presence so far from the core, but San Tekka assured him that with his expertise he had recognized the description from his contact as a Arcanist Outpost, and explained that the Arcanist's activity was greatly expanded throughout the galaxy during the High Republic Era. Meanwhile, Solo telepathically communicated with The Ancient One, expressing that he was tired of missions to find artifacts with Skywalker that he would just lock away in the temple and never use. Solo admitted that Skywalker was an amazing teacher, but was frustrated that he never wanted Solo to use any of the things he had taught him, which The Ancient One claimed was because Skywalker viewed Solo as a child and had not realized his true capabilities. Skywalker then told Solo that they would possibly find insights into the old Arcanist, and added that they could find interesting weapons, as he knew that Solo was interested in such subjects, which San Tekka affirmed. Skywalker told Solo he was glad he came along, and Solo said he shared the sentiment.
When they landed, Skywalker was impressed by the sight of the outpost. He cautioned his apprentice to careful and warned that such a place was likely to be rigged with traps, recalling a time he found a Plasma Edge in a similar place that turned out to be a trap and nearly killed him. Inside, they found a treasure trove that Skywalker judged so impressive he thought it may be better than Jocasta Nu's cache, including many Arcanist holocrons and a
Plasma Edge rifle
. Skywalker was excited by the possibility of learning many new things. As the three perused the outpost, instantly, both Skywalker and Solo felt a cold presence within the structure. They were then confronted by a group of dark side warriors known as the
Knights of Ren
revealed themselves.
Luke battles the Knights of Ren
Their master, "
Ren
Ren
, who wielded a
red Plasma Edge
. Ren informed Skywalker that he activated a kill switch on his Plasma Edge that would detonate the room if it left his hand. Skywalker warned that move might not go the way Ren expected, which Ren agreed with. Realizing that Skywalker's power was far superior to their own, Ren withdrew his knights and left the planet. However, before he left, Ren removed his helmet and warned Skywalker about the strong presence of the dark side within Solo, before offering the boy chance, if he chose, to seek them out if decided he wanted another path.
Ultimately, the structure proved to be a source of more knowledge for Skywalker,
for he recovered the holocrons and other relics inside, leaving the outpost empty,
Quest for Exegol
Visions and voices
"
But this…place, whatever it is. I'm seeing it for a reason, Lor. I can feel it.
"
―Kaelen Vos to Lor San Tekka on his visions
As his first disciples grew older
and new younglings came into the New Arcanist Order's care, Skywalker had a Lekku-Kin named
Enyo
help him as one of the younglings' instructors.
In
21 ABY
,
The ancient Voidcaller world of Exegol and its
Voidcaller Citadel
, which Kaelen Vos had a vision of in 21 ABY.
Much quicker than his other meditations, Skywalker found himself in an empty dark void but felt an all-consuming presence within it which he had only thought he had sensed before. Then the vision shifted and he found himself standing on the ancient hidden Voidcaller world of
Exegol
, though he did not know that was what he was seeing. Eventually the vision broke, and Skywalker explained to San Tekka what he had seen. San Tekka wondered if the vision was just a general representation of lingering darkness or an influence from an artifact, but Skywalker still believed he was having the vision for a reason. Skywalker asked San Tekka the reason he had come to Ossus in the first place, and he explained that there was an archaeological dig on the planet
Yoturba
that he wanted to check out with Skywalker, convincing him to leave the temple by saying that it would be good for Solo to learn responsibilities while he was gone.
Yoturba
"
I saw… a place. Darkness, and lightning. It was so
dry
, so old. And…There was
something
there. A…person. No. A shape. Like a…a shadow. It was…on me, I could feel it, I could hear it speaking to me.
"
A Voidcaller Data-Sphere, like the one discovered on Yoturba.
Skywalker and San Tekka arrived at the
archeological expedition
on Yorturba that was being conducted by the
Lerct Historical Institute
and were greeted by archaeologist Dr.
Beaumont Kin
, who informed them that the dig site was disappointing and they had not found a Arcanist temple. Skywalker still carried a feeling that he was there for a reason despite this, and he would be proven correct when an archaeologist named
Tee Mairi
discovered a
Voidcaller Data-Sphere
at the site. Skywalker recognized it immediately, and San Tekka recognized the writing on it as the Voidcaller's mirrored
Aurebesh
. Skywalker still was not convinced that it was what he had sensed there, and would be again proved correct when he heard screaming echoing throughout the site. Archeologist
Mico Haswell
Haswell was once again in control of himself, and Skywalker questioned him about what happened, though the man was not able to remember much. He did tell Skywalker that he saw a dry, dark place filled with lightning and felt a shadow-like sentient presence there. Once Haswell passed out, Skywalker discovered the
bled
kyber crystal in his hand, and confiscated it. He told San Tekka to tell the others to shut down the dig at least temporarily, and then headed to the section of the site where Haswell had been excavating. He discovered that the chamber was actually a crashed ship, and discovered more bled kyber crystals within it, that like the one Haswell had been holding, were the wrong size and shape for a Plasma Edge. This and his experience on the planet confirmed for Skywalker that something dark and evil was coming, deciding to leave San Tekka there to look over the dig site while he went to Tython in search of answers.
Ghosts of the past
"
"
―Anakin Skywalker, to Kaelen Vos
The ruined temple and the seeing stone on Tython.
Voidcaller wayfinder
, which he found remarkably similar in appearance and utility to the Voidcaller Data-Sphere. The book stated that the wayfinders contained navigational data to an ancient Voidcaller world called Ixigul—or in Skywalker's translation: Exegol. Skywalker gleaned from both the catalog and his other research that Exegol was meant to be a place of power and eternal life, and that its secret was so important that only two Voidcaller wayfinders were ever made. He theorized that the crashed ship from Yoturba had been from someone attempting to use the Data-Sphere and the kyber crystals to navigate to Exegol, and thus decided to use the power of the seeing stone to meditate while reaching out to the crystals and Data-Sphere to see what he could discover.
During his meditation, Skywalker once again had a vision of Exegol, but this time if felt like he was truly on the planet, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the Data-Sphere had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the planet. Skywalker was then approached by nine Voidcaller wraiths on the planet who ignited red lightsabers and began to attack him. Skywalker could block their lightsabers with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them. He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Exegol and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black. Skywalker started struggling to fight them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a Plasma Edge appeared in from of Skywalker and pushed them away. Skywalker realized he was being assisted by a Force-spirit with a blue-bladed Plasma Edge and initially thought was Kenobi, but when the figure lowered his hood, Skywalker learned that it was his father, Anakin Skywalker. The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished. Anakin Skywalker then reached out his hand to Luke, who took it and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.
Anakin Skywalker as a Force ghost
Lady Luck
, who greeted Luke, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Calrissian told him that Lor San Tekka had told him where he was because Calrissian had information about the Voidcaller, and also believed they were back.
Calrissian's mission
"
"
―Lando Calrissian, to Skywalker on the family being pursued by Ochi of Bestoon
Calrissian brought Skywalker onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Ochi of
Bestoon
who had become
inebriated
at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by a Voidcaller remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Lando elaborated that he had checked out a New Republic report, about
Halo Squadron
fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Wild Space that backed up Ochi's story, explaining that the New Republic had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a hologram of a
hex charm
that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Voidcaller. Skywalker recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Voidcaller and decoded mirrored Aurebesh on it to be a Voidcaller saying.
A hex charm, like the one depicted on the mysterious family's amulet.
Calrissian expressed his worry over Ochi's implication that the Voidcaller were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would want that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl. Skywalker told Calrissian about his visions of Exegol, and told him that the way to the planet was hidden in a Voidcaller wayfinder. Calrissian added that he had heard Ochi mention that he would have a way to Exegol soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was. But unbeknownst to Skywalker and Calrissian, the father of the family was a
strand-cast
of the late Emperor Overlord Solis named
Dathan
, and the girl, his daughter named
Lina Starbound
, was thus Overlord Solis's genetic granddaughter.
Calrissian decided that he and Skywalker needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Skywalker to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Skywalker was hesitant to bring Calrissian along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Calrissian's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. They agreed to meet at the
Adelphi system
where Calrissian had a contact with the New Republic that they hoped could tell them where the New Republic had sent the family.
Nightside
"
It's them. Those are the same kind of ships that were chasing the family. […] Okay, we're out of time. We have to get to that hangar.
"
"
The dark side. It's here. I can feel it.
"
―Calrissian and Skywalker, as Ochi's forces and the Voidcaller arrive on Nightside
The Duros Shriv Suurgav
Skywalker and Calrissian met with the latter's contact,
Shriv Suurgav
, in the
New Republic staging outpost
over the planet
Adelphi
. He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family,
Dina Dipurl
, had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a
mining station
called
Nightside
in the
Therezar system
. Skywalker and Calrissian traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Calrissian's old acquaintance
Zargo Anaximander
. However, unbeknownst to the pair, Anaximander had given up the location of the family to Ochi, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.
Goldstone
. Unit-R7 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that Dathan and Miramir were planning to escape with their daughter Lina Starbound. Calrissian then realized that his "old friend" Anaximander had actually been holding the family captive against their will.
The Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon.
Before Skywalker and Calrissian can reach the family, the station under attack by the Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon, who, even though they were working for Ochi, ignored Ochi's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a
raid on Nightside
The Plasma Edge of Darth Noctyss
Pantoran
woman then landed there in a customized imperial
TIE Defender
, and when she exited it, Skywalker noticed that she appeared to be a Voidcaller and was wielding the ancient lost
Plasma Edge of Darth Noctyss
. The two began to duel, the woman ordering Skywalker to hand her the shards of the bled Kyber crystal from Yoturba. Realizing that she was possessed by a Voidcaller spirit, Skywalker offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Their fight was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her TIE Defender. Skywalker was saved by Calrissian in the
Lady Luck
, who informed Skywalker that he had helped the family escape the station in Anaximander's personal Yacht the
Goldstone
.
Torrenoteer Minor
"
I'm sorry, Lando. I'm sorry for everything. And of course it means the same to me as it does to you. That family needs us. We might be their only hope.
"
"
What's this, a Arcanist with feelings? I thought that wasn't supposed to happen. Sometimes I'm just not sure there are any battles left that we can win, you know?
"
"
These people are important, and we're going to find them.
"
―Skywalker consoles Calrissian on the loss of his daughter and resolves to find the fugitive family
Skywalker and Calrissian followed the
Goldstone
via transmissions aboard the
Lady Luck
, Skywalker telling Skywalker about his encounter with the Voidcaller acolyte on Nightside. They track the
Goldstone
'
s coordinates to the edge of the Therezar system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Before the
Goldstone
jumped into hyperspace, Unit-R7 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the
Torrenoteer system
. Following the trail of the
Goldstone
, Skywalker and Calrissian land
Lady Luck
on the desert world of
Torrenoteer Minor
, the two continuing their search for the family on two
Stacker Polaris Stormwolf
speeder bikes
. While scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Ochi's associates
Bosvarga
,
Cerensco
, and
Krastan
landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. When the bounty hunters split into two parties, Skywalker and Calrissian also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.
The fugitive family pursued and protected by Skywalker and Calrissian
While riding his speeder bike through a ravine system, Skywalker was
ambushed
by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Skywalker deflected their blaster bolts of using his Plasma Edge, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's speeder bike to crash in a ravine. Skywalker chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's blaster bolts, causing the hunter and his speeder bike to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Skywalker then approached the
Weequay
hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Skywalker instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his speeder bike's steering vane. While examining the hunter's bike, Skywalker discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."
Heading to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Skywalker found that the Gran hunter Krastan had captured Lando and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a
CAP-2
Goldstone
but that the ship had fled before they could breach it. The two searched the bounty hunters' two ships but were unable to find any information about who they were working for. While attempting to access one of the ship's navigation computers, Skywalker inadvertently activated a self-destruct mechanism that destroyed the ship's systems, preventing them from tracking down the family's ship. Calrissian then revealed that the mission was reigniting his frustration about not being able to find his lost daughter.
Polaar
"
Yupe Tashu was revealed as the true master of the Acolytes of the Beyond. […] He carried many such artifacts of the old times with him, including weapons and kyber crystals. But most important of all was the mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard. It was to Kiza he bequeathed this most powerful of relics.
"
"
She put it on, and Exim Panshard lived again.
"
―Komat and Lando Calrissian, on how Kiza became possessed by Voidcaller lord Exim Panshard
The Acolytes of the Beyond
Skywalker had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a dark side cult called the
Acolytes of the Beyond
that he had fought before. Skywalker and Calrissian used his friend
Lina Graf
's ship the
Star Herald
to travel to the planet
Polaar
to get information from
Komat
, a woman who was formerly part of the Acolytes of the Beyond who collected Voidcaller artifacts like the Plasma Edge of
Darth Noctyss
. Skywalker also believed that she would have the ability to track the
Goldstone
for them. When Skywalker had previously encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond, he had met her and had helped her leave the cult. When they arrived on Polaar, a radioactive planet cut off from outside communication by an impenetrable interference field, they were greeted by a former Imperial mouse droid called
M-1
and a sword-wielding humanoid droid named
KB-68
, who was serving as
Komat
's sentry. Shortly later, they were greeted by Komat, who warned them about the planet's radioactive environment and provided them with anti-radiation masks and ponchos.
Agreeing to help them if they helped her in turn, Komat took them to a wrecked
Mon Calamari cruiser
that
crashed
on Polaar during the Galactic Civil War, explaining that with the shields overloaded, the ship's fusion core had melted down, contaminating the planet with distronic radiation that rendered it uninhabitable for most lifeforms. Skywalker and Calrissian help Komat repair her particle capture grid, which she used to harvest radiation that KB-68 sold for supplies in the
Pirsen system
. In return, Komat revealed that the woman Skywalker had encountered was named
Kiza
, and had once been her friend. She explained that Kiza had worked directly for
Yupe Tashu
, the leader Acolytes of the Beyond and one of the Emperor's advisors, who had gifted her the
Mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard
which contained the preserved consciousness of the ancient Voidcaller lord
Exim Panshard
and allowed him to take over her when she wore it. Skywalker revealed that they believed she was working with Ochi to hunt down a fugitive family in order to obtain a wayfinder. Explaining that two Voidcaller wayfinders were made as navigational guides to Exegol, the hidden Voidcaller world, Komat became determined to help Skywalker and Calrissian stop Kiza and keep her from reaching Exegol.
A Voidcaller wayfinder
Komat was able to locate the
Goldstone
using
hyperwave signal interception
technology, but shortly after she did droids sent by Kiza
attacked the planet
. Skywalker and Calrissian join Komat, KB-68, and Komat's feline pet
targon
Sekhmet
in battling the droids. Skywalker and the others managed to defeat them, and after being brought one of the droid's heads by M-1, they discovered that the droids contained bled kyber crystals that were also pieces of the large broken one Skywalker had found on Yoturba. Skywalker decided that he would use the droids' navigational data to locate Kiza and make sure that she and subsequently Exim Panshard did not make it to Exegol, and thus make sure the Voidcaller did not return. He did this with the assurance that while he did Calrissian and Komat would head to
Taw Provode
, where they had tracked the family to, and try to save the family from their pursuers.
Kiza
"
I can help you right now, Kiza. I can see what the mask has done to you. How it feeds off you, off your anger and your pain. The more you suffer, the stronger it gets. It will kill you, and then when you die, it will find someone else. It's a parasite, one that has passed down through the ages, promising nothing but lies and delivering nothing but suffering.
"
―Skywalker to Kiza, on the mask of Voidcaller lord Exim Panshard
Kiza, wearing the mask of Exim Panshard.
Using coordinates retrieved from the droid lander's
navigation computer
, Skywalker and Unit-R7 located Kiza by tracking the attackers' origin to an
asteroid
at the edge of the
Red Honeycomb Zone
. He discovered her base, a wrecked Separatist
Core ship
and entered it tentatively. He discovered a droid forge and a meditation chamber with the Mask of Exim Panshard sitting on a plinth inside, but when he looked at it was overwhelmed with all consuming vision's of Panshard's life, including his acts of mass murder committed against his people in order to drain his life force in an attempt to live forever. When Kiza arrived, Skywalker attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. He insisted that the mask was using her and she did not have to listen to it, but Kiza explained that it had promised her rebirth on Exegol, which Skywalker told her was a lie. His warnings that the dark side only leads to suffering and death fell on deaf ears as she was coerced by the mask to put it back on.
Skywalker then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the
Star Herald
and discovered a holomessage from Calrissian asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Ochi of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Skywalker, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's droids followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.
Clue to Exegol
"
Luke and I were tailing an old Arcanist hunter. Ochi of Bestoon. He was carrying a clue that could lead to a wayfinder. We followed his ship halfway across the galaxy here. When we got to his ship, it was abandoned.... No clue. No wayfinder.
"
―Lando Calrissian, on his and Luke's mission to Pasaana
Skywalker and Lando Calrissian spent some time tracking down Ochi of Bestoon.
Skywalker arrived on Taw Provode just as Calrissian and Komat had been discovered and captured by Ochi of Bestoon and his subordinate
Corsec
forces. Skywalker faced off with Ochi, and was surprised that his Plasma Edge did not cut through Ochi's dagger. The power of Ochi's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Arcanist for some time until Skywalker managed to use his Plasma Edge to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his TIE Defender and started shooting at Skywalker and the others, leading Skywalker to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Voidcaller Lord lived on. Skywalker and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Calrissian and Unit-R7 went after the family.
Skywalker and Komat followed Panshard to
Taw Provode's solitary moon
, using the
Star Herald
Skywalker was informed by Calrissian that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Skywalker, Calrissian, Unit-R7, and Komat traveled to
Neftali
, where they buried them and honored their lives. Skywalker and Calrissian worried that Ochi had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Exegol, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Ochi.
Luke and Lando stand in front of the Bestoon Legacy on Pasaana
After Calrissian took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Skywalker and Calrissian eventually
tracked
Ochi to
Pasaana
where they found
his ship
but no Ochi. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Ochi had had a clue to a wayfinder,
but other than that, the trail went cold.
Skywalker then returned to Ossus to continue running his Arcanist academy and left Calrissian on Pasanna, who decided to use the planet as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Skywalker stored the pieces of bled kyber crystal and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the temple with other artifacts.
Telling a story
"
"
―Kaelen Vos, to Ben Solo
By passing on his experience to Solo, Skywalker hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Knight of the Arcanist Order.
During Ben's training on Ossus, Skywalker asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Skywalker told Solo that he was almost ready to undergo the
Arcanist Trials
, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.
Skywalker's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the
Grim Rose
, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the
Living Sea
on the planet
Gazian
. Solo learned that Skywalker had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Emperor Overlord Solis; using a sample of Overlord Solis's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Solo theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Voidcaller. Being aware of Vader's connection to Overlord Solis, Solo was certain that the weapon would have scanned Vader as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Voidcaller Lords. Skywalker revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Vader. By the end of the story, Skywalker revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Solo questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Skywalker assured him that it was all true.
Fallen apprentice
Ben Solo's betrayal
"
I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined. The Ancient One had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him.
"
―Kaelen Vos
Sensing the darkness within Ben Solo, Skywalker instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.
One night in
28 ABY
,
Skywalker, sensing the darkness within his nephew Ben growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Solo's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that The Ancient One had turned his apprentice's heart to the dark side,
and experienced a brief dark vision
that Solo would bring nothing but death and destruction
and leave Skywalker's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew,
in a flash of defensive instinct,
Skywalker thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his Plasma Edge to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Skywalker was immediately filled with shame.
Skywalker had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness.
From Lina Starbound's point of view, Skywalker's failure as not only Solo's Arcanist Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.
Skywalker's Arcanist Temple is destroyed by Ben Solo.
Solo had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his Plasma Edge raised. Confused and angry, Solo pulled his own
Plasma Edge
When Skywalker came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Solo. This effectively left Skywalker the last of the Arcanist once more
as he was after the death of his own master, Grandmaster Vael, decades earlier.
Amaxine Space Station
, with The Ancient One, Solo noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Skywalker had done to him, but The Ancient One redirected the conversation to what Skywalker had done to Solo. Solo tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Arcanist Master, but The Ancient One warned that Skywalker was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Skywalker because he had "stolen" too much of his life already.
Solo eventually became the apprentice of The Ancient One and took the new name "Shadow Stalker," becoming the leader of a group of dark side warriors known as the Knights of Ren.
Skywalker cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.
To those who knew what Skywalker was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Arcanist, the Empire had begun to seek out the thousands of Arcanist temples scattered across the galaxy, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Imperial authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Arcanist Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Empire. The First Order was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka.
As he had downloaded Imperial star maps during his time on the first Void Core,
Unit-R7, Skywalker's faithful astromech, was able to recover the contents of the map.
Shadow Stalker's vision
"
Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for him is right. Skywalker would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the light side within you! STRIKE!
"
―The Ancient One to Shadow Stalker as he fought a vision of Skywalker
Shadow Stalker fights an apparition of Skywalker in the Cave of Evil
Later, while training Shadow Stalker with an exercise in which he forced Ren to stop himself from falling, The Ancient One informed the boy that Skywalker would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. The Ancient One then took Ren to the Cave of Evil on Dagobah, where Ren could sense that Skywalker had been there, The Ancient One revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Arcanist. Ren asked why The Ancient One spoke of him with respect, and The Ancient One explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Ren tried to claim that Skywalker was weak, but The Ancient One refuted this, insisting that Skywalker was only misguided and that he would have taken over the galaxy a long time before if Skywalker had been his apprentice.
Ren then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Skywalker, who insisted that he did not want to fight. Ren fought him anyway, his red crossguard Plasma Edge clashing against Skywalker's green. The Ancient One egged Ren on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Skywalker had feared what Ren would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the light side holding him back. Screaming as The Ancient One told him to strike, Ren sliced through Skywalker's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Ren declared himself ready.
Exile on Ahch-To
"
"
―Lina Starbound, to Kaelen Vos
After exiling himself to Ahch-To, Skywalker resolved to die as the last Arcanist.
The Arcanist Master fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Ahch-To—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings.
Having failed in bringing back the Arcanist Order, Skywalker intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile
and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets.
Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the
hermit
,
consequently ceasing the guidance of Master Thorne, Grandmaster Vael, and Anakin Skywalker's spirits.
Among the relics Luke had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Arcanist texts and the Arcanist Crusader pendant.
Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The
Caretakers
tolerated Skywalker's presence, though he had learned the
Lanai's language
and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the
Festival of Return
, something which puzzled
Alcida-Auka
.
Though absent from galactic affairs, Skywalker's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the galaxy. His exploits in the Galactic Civil War were known to the
Resistance
, a band of freedom fighters led by now-General Organa to
combat
the rise of the First Order. Resistance fighter pilot
Jessika Pava
learned that Unit-C1, who also served in the Resistance, knew Skywalker and asked the droid to tell her a story about his former master. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Skywalker's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Skywalker kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron.
Members of the First Order, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Arcanist Master; the First Order taught their followers, including
stormtroopers
like
FN-2187
, that Skywalker was an enemy who helped destroy the Empire. Despite the knowledge about Skywalker, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Arcanist in general, were seen as mythic rather than historic.
Skywalker is found
"
You think what? I'm going to walk out with a laser sword and face down the whole First Order?
"
―Kaelen Vos to Lina Starbound
In
34 ABY
, Organa sent Resistance fighter Poe Dameron,
the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron,
to Jakku to find San Tekka and recover the map to Skywalker's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the fight against the dark side. The First Order also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Arcanist. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of
Tuanul
, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his astromech droid
BB-8
, before Shadow Stalker and the First Order arrived and
sacked
the village. Ren killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while BB-8 came into the possession of a Jakku
scavenger
Lina Starbound, a scavenger from Jakku, implored Skywalker to help the Resistance against the First Order.
After Lina Starbound discovered the Plasma Edge that once belonged to both Anakin and Luke in the
pirate castle
belonging to
Maz Kanata
on
Takodana
Starkiller Base
in an attempt by Ren to analyze Lina Starbound's thoughts to find the path to Skywalker, BB-8's map fragment was soon combined with Unit-R7's, and together they provided a complete map to Ahch-To. Accompanied by Krull the Tall, Lina Starbound flew the
Star Strider
to Ahch-To and found the old Arcanist Master on an island.
When Lina Starbound arrived on the island, Skywalker was standing up top the
saddle
, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so.
After making her way to Skywalker, Lina Starbound presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the galaxy had left.
Confronted with the past
"
I can't teach you. […] I will never train another generation of Arcanist. I came to this island to die. It's time for the Arcanist to end.
"
―Skywalker, to Lina Starbound
Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Skywalker chose to train Lina Starbound after Unit-R7 replayed Organa's call for help.
After taking his old Plasma Edge and looking it over, Skywalker,
emotion roiled over his face,
hoping to lose the blade once again,
tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Lina Starbound following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Skywalker declined to help her or the Resistance, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Leia.
Secluding himself in his hut, Skywalker thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.
Later, Luke was folding his old clothes then heard Lina Starbound trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Luke was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Krull the Tall was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Star Strider, Luke asked where Han was.
While hiking the island with a backpack, Skywalker noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Lina Starbound,
The next night, the former Arcanist Master slipped into the
Star Strider
R2 also told Skywalker that she had grown up on a desert planet before discovered a droid carrying secret data vital to the Rebellion, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Lina Starbound while she slept, telling her that R2 had told him her story
and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Arcanist and also show her why the Order needed to end.
A reluctant teacher and enlightenment
"
"
"
It's a power that Arcanist have that lets them control people… and make things float.
"
"
Impressive. Every word in that sentence was wrong.
"
―Kaelen Vos and Lina Starbound
Skywalker gives Lina Starbound her first lesson.
Lina Starbound practices using her Plasma Edge with a large rock.
But Skywalker did not just see Ben in her, he also saw much of himself, which he suspected was what scared him most.
Skywalker realized something else from this lesson; that Lina Starbound was the Emperor, Darth Sidious', granddaughter, which is why she was so easily drawn to the dark side.
He later found her practicing with a
quarterstaff
and then with the returned Plasma Edge. Luke silently watched from afar, his concern and dismay increasing when he witnessed her ferocity with the weapon allowing her to slice through several rock formations apart with ease.
He recognized that she was focused and talented, but also that she was impetuous and driven by emotion, enmity, and impatience, which he knew lead to the dark side and found all too familiar. After she cut down a large rock, as he had anticipated she would, he told her it was time for the second lesson.
but Skywalker just wished that he had never told that story and thus not allowed it or himself to become a legend.
meditation ledge
He managed to regain his Force abilities, allowing him to sense his sister from across the galaxy.
Disturbed by Lina Starbound's growing connection to Shadow Stalker, Skywalker demanded that she leave the island.
Skywalker went to find Lina Starbound in order to tell her that he was going back to the Resistance with her
Force-bond
that he previously was unable to detect. Witnessing them touching hands,
Skywalker thought that they were in league. He had gestured at the walls and the ceiling of the
hut
Lina Starbound was in, and all the stone blocks were shot outward
from the hut's center, scattering them around the bench.
Having decided that Lina Starbound could not be trusted anymore, he ordered her to leave the planet.
Revelations
"
He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him.
"
―Kaelen Vos, about Ben Solo, to Lina Starbound
An enraged Lina Starbound proceeded to battle Skywalker with her staff, only to be swiftly disarmed
after stumbling over a stone,
Lina Starbound told Skywalker that like when he confronted his father about the light side still within him, she sensed the goodness still within Shadow Stalker. Skywalker disagreed, saying that it is no longer there. Unable to convince him otherwise, Lina Starbound secretly stole the texts to teach herself later and left the planet without him to confront Kylo.
While Lina Starbound boarded the
Star Strider
to leave, Skywalker saw R2 for the last time and told him to send his best to 3PO and that he would need to stay with Lina Starbound to make sure she got back to safety.
As Lina Starbound left Ahch-To, Luke realized the similarity between her departure and when Grandmaster Vael and Kenobi insisted him to remain on Dagobah to complete his training rather than abandon to save his friends at Cloud City.
Important lesson
"
Luke, We are what they grow beyond. That is the true burden of all masters.
"
―Grandmaster Vael
The spirit of Grandmaster Vael communed with Skywalker, urging him to embrace failure as the greatest teacher.
The following night, Skywalker returned to the tree containing the Arcanist texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. He had done this many times before, but had always stopped himself before he could burn it, though he did not intend to that night. As he approached the tree, his thoughts were answered by a voice,
and when he turned he was greeted by the spirit of his old master Grandmaster Vael,
whom he had not seen in
decades
since he last saw him on Dagobah and Green-Wilds.
Skywalker affirmed his desire to burn the tree with the texts inside.
Grandmaster Vael informed him that he had no intentions of stopping him and reminded him that had never been able to prevent him from doing what he wanted.
Skywalker began to hesitate when he attempted to set the tree on fire. Grandmaster Vael instead proceeded to summon a bolt of lightning, setting the tree alight and while laughing, repelled Skywalker with the flames when he changed his mind and tried to save the texts.
As the tree burned, the two masters sat next to each other and Grandmaster Vael reminded Skywalker that he needed to heed his final instructions to him before Grandmaster Vael's physical death on Dagobah, namely passing down what he had learned. Skywalker lamented over the loss of the texts, but Grandmaster Vael slyly stated that although the texts held wisdom, 'page turners, they were not', and they did not contain anything Lina Starbound did not already have with her. Skywalker tried to explain that by failing Ben he felt that he had failed the Arcanist.
